@app.get("/api/place")
async def place_search(q: str):
    q=q.strip()
    if len(q) > 120:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Place search must be 120 characters or fewer.")
    if not q:
        return JSONResponse({"results":[]})
    log_phase("PLACE", "Searching location", query=q)
    for k,v in COMMON_PLACES.items():
        if q.lower()==k.lower():
            result={"display_name":k+", preset",**v,"source":"preset","timezone_needs_confirmation":False}
            log_phase("PLACE", "Preset location matched", place=k, timezone=v["timezone"])
            return JSONResponse({"results":[result]})
    url="https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?"+urlencode({"format":"jsonv2","limit":6,"q":q})
    try:
        req=Request(url,headers={"User-Agent":"JyotishLab/0.9.8 (+local-user-tool)"})
        with urlopen(req,timeout=8) as r:
            raw = r.read(512_001)
            if len(raw) > 512_000:
                raise ValueError("Geocoder response exceeded the size limit")
            data=json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
        tf=TimezoneFinder() if TimezoneFinder else None
        results=[]
        for x in data:
            lat=float(x["lat"]); lon=float(x["lon"])
            tz=tf.timezone_at(lat=lat, lng=lon) if tf else None
            results.append({"display_name":x.get("display_name",""),"latitude":lat,"longitude":lon,"timezone":tz or "UTC","timezone_needs_confirmation":not bool(tz),"source":"nominatim"})
        log_phase("PLACE", "Geocoder returned results", count=len(results), timezone_resolved=sum(1 for x in results if not x["timezone_needs_confirmation"]))
        return JSONResponse({"results":results})
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("[PLACE] Geocoder request failed (%s)", type(exc).__name__)
        return JSONResponse({"results":[],"warning":"Geocoder unavailable. Enter coordinates manually if needed."})

def _birth_from_payload(payload: BirthRequest):
    y,m,d=map(int,payload.dob.split("-")); amount=payload.forecast_amount
    if payload.forecast_years is not None and payload.forecast_amount==5 and payload.forecast_unit=="years": amount=payload.forecast_years
    return BirthData(payload.name,date(y,m,d),payload.tob,payload.latitude,payload.longitude,payload.timezone,payload.place),amount

@app.post("/api/muhurta/search")
async def api_muhurta_search(payload: MuhurtaRequest):
    try:
        natal_moon = None
        if payload.natal_dob and payload.natal_tob:
            from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
            from .engine import norm360
            import swisseph as swe
            birth_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(payload.natal_dob + "T" + payload.natal_tob).replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo(payload.natal_timezone or payload.timezone))
            utc_dt = birth_dt.astimezone(__import__('datetime').timezone.utc)
            jd = swe.julday(utc_dt.year, utc_dt.month, utc_dt.day, utc_dt.hour + utc_dt.minute/60 + utc_dt.second/3600)
            swe.set_sid_mode(swe.SIDM_LAHIRI)
            natal_moon = norm360(swe.calc_ut(jd, swe.MOON, swe.FLG_SWIEPH | swe.FLG_SIDEREAL)[0][0])
        from .muhurta import search_muhurta
        return search_muhurta(activity=payload.activity, start_date=payload.start_date, end_date=payload.end_date,
            latitude=payload.latitude, longitude=payload.longitude, timezone_name=payload.timezone,
            natal_moon_longitude=natal_moon)
    except ValueError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e))
    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception("[MUHURTA] Search failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Muhurta calculation failed. Check the request and server logs.")


@app.post("/api/personal/analyze")
async def api_personal(payload: BirthRequest):
    started=time.perf_counter()
    log_phase("PERSONAL", "Starting Kundali calculation", name=payload.name, dob=payload.dob, place=payload.place, school=payload.school, forecast=f"{payload.forecast_amount} {payload.forecast_unit}")
    try:
        birth,amount=_birth_from_payload(payload)
        log_phase("PERSONAL", "Birth data normalized", timezone=payload.timezone, latitude=payload.latitude, longitude=payload.longitude)
        log_phase("PERSONAL", "Running astronomical/chart engine")
        chart=analyze_birth(birth,forecast_amount=amount,forecast_unit=payload.forecast_unit,forecast_granularity=payload.forecast_granularity, school=payload.school, forecast_start_date=payload.forecast_start_date, forecast_end_date=payload.forecast_end_date)
        log_phase("PERSONAL", "Chart engine complete", planets=len(chart.get("planets",{})), vargas=len(chart.get("divisional_charts",{})), forecast_rows=len(chart.get("transits",[])))
        chart["meta"]["name"]=payload.name
        from .validation import personal_calculation_audit
        log_phase("PERSONAL", "Running calculation audit")
        chart["validation_audit"]=personal_calculation_audit(chart)
        log_phase("PERSONAL", "Generating narrative")
        narrative=generate_narrative(chart)
        ai_result=generate_ai_interpretation(chart,narrative)
        narrative['ai_interpretation']=ai_result
        chart['ai_interpretation']=ai_result
        log_phase("PERSONAL", "AI interpretation stage complete", status=ai_result.get('status'))
        log_phase("PERSONAL", "Kundali request complete", duration_ms=round((time.perf_counter()-started)*1000,1))
        return {"chart":chart,"narrative":narrative}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception("[PERSONAL] Calculation failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500,detail="Calculation failed. Check the request and server logs.")


@app.post("/api/personal/chat")
async def api_personal_chat(payload: PersonalChatRequest):
    started=time.perf_counter()
    question=payload.question
    try:
        birth_payload=payload.birth
        birth,amount=_birth_from_payload(birth_payload)
        log_phase('CHAT','Building chart context',name=birth_payload.name,question=question[:120])
        cached=_cached_chat_chart(birth_payload.name,birth_payload.dob,birth_payload.tob,birth_payload.latitude,birth_payload.longitude,birth_payload.timezone,birth_payload.place,amount,birth_payload.forecast_unit,birth_payload.forecast_granularity,birth_payload.school,birth_payload.forecast_start_date,birth_payload.forecast_end_date)
        chart=copy.deepcopy(cached)
        chart['meta']['name']=birth_payload.name
        narrative=generate_narrative(chart)
        result=generate_ai_chat(chart,narrative,question,[m.model_dump() for m in payload.history])
        log_phase('CHAT','Answer ready',status=result.get('status'),duration_ms=round((time.perf_counter()-started)*1000,1))
        return result
    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception('[CHAT] Failed')
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500,detail='Chat failed. Check the request and server logs.')

@app.post("/api/library/analyze")
async def api_library_analyze(payload: LibraryAnalyzeRequest):
    try:
        p=get_person(payload.name)
        # Noon is a clearly labelled reference time when public birth time is unavailable.
        tob=p.get("tob") or "12:00:00"
        birth=BirthData(p["name"],date.fromisoformat(p["dob"]),tob,p["latitude"],p["longitude"],p["timezone"],p["place"])
        chart=analyze_birth(birth,forecast_amount=payload.forecast_amount,forecast_unit=payload.forecast_unit,school=payload.school)
        chart["meta"]["reference_profile"]={"category":p["category"],"time_status":p["time_status"],"source":p["source"],"note":"No verified public birth time is stored; chart uses 12:00 local as a reference and should not be treated as a verified timed Kundali."}
        chart["meta"]["name"]=p["name"]
        return {"profile":p,"chart":chart,"narrative":generate_narrative(chart)}
    except Exception as e: raise HTTPException(status_code=400,detail="Reference profile calculation failed. Check the selected profile and server logs.")

@app.get("/api/mundane/world-events/status")
async def api_world_event_status():
    return world_event_status()

@app.post("/api/mundane/world-events/sync")
async def api_world_event_sync(request: FastAPIRequest):
    started=time.perf_counter()
    expected = os.getenv("WORLD_EVENTS_SYNC_TOKEN", "").strip()
    supplied = request.headers.get("x-admin-token", "")
    if not expected:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="World-event sync is disabled. Configure WORLD_EVENTS_SYNC_TOKEN to enable the administrative action.")
    if not supplied or not hmac.compare_digest(supplied, expected):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Administrative token is missing or invalid.")
    try:
        from subprocess import run
        script=BASE / "scripts" / "sync_world_events.py"
        log_phase("WORLD-EVENTS", "Starting source sync", target=1000)
        proc=run([sys.executable, str(script)], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90, cwd=str(BASE))
        if proc.returncode != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(proc.stderr.strip() or proc.stdout.strip() or "world-event sync failed")
        loaded=reload_world_events()
        log_phase("WORLD-EVENTS", "World historical corpus loaded", events=loaded, duration_ms=round((time.perf_counter()-started)*1000,1))
        return {**world_event_status(),"message":"World historical corpus synchronized."}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception("[WORLD-EVENTS] Sync failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500,detail="World historical sync failed. Check server logs for details.")

def _combine_country_leader_forecasts(country_forecast, leader_chart, time_status, source):
    """Overlay leader timing as a separate corroboration layer; never replace national chart."""
    start = country_forecast.get("start")
    end = country_forecast.get("long_end")
    timing_windows = leader_chart.get("timing_windows", []) or []
    country_rows = (country_forecast.get("near_term", {}).get("monthly", []) or []) + (country_forecast.get("long_term", {}).get("monthly", []) or [])
    # Match date proximity only where a country event indication and leader timing row overlap.
    # Do not interpret absence of overlap as proof of weakness or government failure.
    by_date = {}
    for row in timing_windows:
        d = str(row.get("start", row.get("date", "")))[:10]
        if d: by_date.setdefault(d, []).append(row)
    corroborations=[]
    for row in country_rows:
        d=str(row.get("date", ""))[:10]
        near=[]
        try:
            from datetime import date as _date, timedelta as _td
            dt=_date.fromisoformat(d)
            for offset in (-7,0,7):
                near.extend(by_date.get((dt+_td(days=offset)).isoformat(), []))
        except Exception: pass
        if near and float(row.get("activation", 0) or 0) >= 55:
            corroborations.append({"date":d,"country_event_types":row.get("event_types",row.get("classes",[])),
                                   "country_activation":row.get("activation"),"leader_timing_rows":len(near),
                                   "leader_dasha_lords":sorted({str(x.get("dasha", "")) for x in near if x.get("dasha")}),
                                   "interpretation":"Date-level overlap between country activation and leader Dasha/transit screening windows only; not a validated joint probability."})
    quality = {"verified":"User marked birth time verified","reported":"Reported birth time; provenance should be checked","approximate":"Approximate birth time; houses, Lagna and divisional charts are uncertain","unknown":"Birth time unknown; noon reference chart used, so house-based and divisional conclusions are not reliable"}.get(time_status,"Birth-time quality unverified")
    return {"leader_name":leader_chart.get("meta",{}).get("name") or leader_chart.get("name"),
            "source":source,"birth_time_status":time_status,"data_quality_note":quality,
            "leader_chart":leader_chart,"country_forecast_period":{"start":start,"end":end},
            "corroborations":corroborations[:100],
            "method":"Country chart remains the primary collective chart. Leader natal chart is a separate, secondary timing lens; dates are surfaced as overlaps, not merged into an uncalibrated single score.",
            "interpretation_limits":["A strong individual chart does not by itself prove that a government will be strong or make successful decisions.","If birth time is unknown, noon-reference Lagna, houses and Vargas are not treated as reliable evidence.","Concordance is an astrological research signal, not validated predictive accuracy."]}


@app.post("/api/mundane/analyze")
async def api_mundane(payload: MundaneRequest):
    started=time.perf_counter()
    log_phase("MUNDANE", "Starting country analysis", country=payload.country, historical_limit=payload.historical_limit, near=f"{payload.near_term_amount} {payload.near_term_unit}", long=f"{payload.long_term_amount} {payload.long_term_unit}")
    try:
        log_phase("MUNDANE", "Resolving national chart from corpus")
        analysis=analyze_country(payload.country)
        log_phase("MUNDANE", "National chart resolved", canonical_country=analysis.get("country"), chart_selection=(analysis.get("chart_source") or {}).get("chart_selection", "unknown"))
        log_phase("MUNDANE", "Searching historical analogues")
        history=search_historical_patterns(payload.country,payload.historical_limit,payload.historical_event_types)
        log_phase("MUNDANE", "Historical search complete", matches=len(history.get("results",[])), corpus=history.get("corpus_size"))
        log_phase("MUNDANE", "Scanning forecast horizons")
        forecast=scan_mundane_forecast(payload.country, payload.near_term_amount, payload.long_term_amount, payload.near_term_unit, payload.long_term_unit, period_start_date=payload.period_start_date, period_end_date=payload.period_end_date)
        leader_result = None
        if payload.leader_custom:
            required = [payload.leader_dob, payload.leader_place, payload.leader_latitude, payload.leader_longitude]
            if any(v is None or v == "" for v in required):
                raise ValueError("Custom leader chart requires birth date, birthplace, latitude and longitude")
            from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
            ZoneInfo(payload.leader_timezone)
            leader_name = (payload.leader_name or "Custom country leader").strip()
            leader_tob = payload.leader_tob or "12:00:00"
            leader_birth = BirthData(leader_name, date.fromisoformat(payload.leader_dob), leader_tob,
                                     float(payload.leader_latitude), float(payload.leader_longitude),
                                     payload.leader_timezone, payload.leader_place)
            leader_chart = analyze_birth(leader_birth, forecast_amount=1, forecast_unit="years",
                                         forecast_start_date=payload.period_start_date,
                                         forecast_end_date=payload.period_end_date)
            leader_result = _combine_country_leader_forecasts(forecast, leader_chart, payload.leader_time_status,
                                                               "custom-entered")
        elif payload.leader_name:
            try:
                person = get_person(payload.leader_name)
            except KeyError:
                raise ValueError("Leader is not in the reference database. Choose custom leader details to continue.")
            leader_tob = person.get("tob") or "12:00:00"
            leader_birth = BirthData(person["name"], date.fromisoformat(person["dob"]), leader_tob,
                                     float(person["latitude"]), float(person["longitude"]),
                                     person["timezone"], person["place"])
            leader_chart = analyze_birth(leader_birth, forecast_amount=1, forecast_unit="years",
                                         forecast_start_date=payload.period_start_date,
                                         forecast_end_date=payload.period_end_date)
            leader_result = _combine_country_leader_forecasts(forecast, leader_chart, person.get("time_status", "unknown"), person.get("source", "reference database"))
        if leader_result:
            forecast["leader_country_synthesis"] = leader_result
        log_phase("MUNDANE", "Forecast scan complete", near_rows=len(forecast.get("near_term",{}).get("monthly",[])), long_rows=len(forecast.get("long_term",{}).get("monthly",[])))
        log_phase("MUNDANE", "Country request complete", duration_ms=round((time.perf_counter()-started)*1000,1))
        return {"analysis":analysis,"historical":history,"forecast":forecast,"available_countries":available_countries()}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception("[MUNDANE] Calculation failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500,detail="Mundane calculation failed. Check the request and server logs.")

@app.post("/api/mundane/report.pdf")
async def api_mundane_pdf(payload: MundaneRequest):
    try:
        analysis=analyze_country(payload.country)
        history=search_historical_patterns(payload.country,payload.historical_limit,payload.historical_event_types)
        forecast=scan_mundane_forecast(payload.country, payload.near_term_amount, payload.long_term_amount, payload.near_term_unit, payload.long_term_unit, period_start_date=payload.period_start_date, period_end_date=payload.period_end_date)
        leader_result = None
        if payload.leader_custom:
            required = [payload.leader_dob, payload.leader_place, payload.leader_latitude, payload.leader_longitude]
            if any(v is None or v == "" for v in required):
                raise ValueError("Custom leader chart requires birth date, birthplace, latitude and longitude")
            from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
            ZoneInfo(payload.leader_timezone)
            leader_name = (payload.leader_name or "Custom country leader").strip()
            leader_tob = payload.leader_tob or "12:00:00"
            leader_birth = BirthData(leader_name, date.fromisoformat(payload.leader_dob), leader_tob,
                                     float(payload.leader_latitude), float(payload.leader_longitude),
                                     payload.leader_timezone, payload.leader_place)
            leader_chart = analyze_birth(leader_birth, forecast_amount=1, forecast_unit="years",
                                         forecast_start_date=payload.period_start_date,
                                         forecast_end_date=payload.period_end_date)
            leader_result = _combine_country_leader_forecasts(forecast, leader_chart, payload.leader_time_status,
                                                               "custom-entered")
        elif payload.leader_name:
            try:
                person = get_person(payload.leader_name)
            except KeyError:
                raise ValueError("Leader is not in the reference database. Choose custom leader details to continue.")
            leader_tob = person.get("tob") or "12:00:00"
            leader_birth = BirthData(person["name"], date.fromisoformat(person["dob"]), leader_tob,
                                     float(person["latitude"]), float(person["longitude"]),
                                     person["timezone"], person["place"])
            leader_chart = analyze_birth(leader_birth, forecast_amount=1, forecast_unit="years",
                                         forecast_start_date=payload.period_start_date,
                                         forecast_end_date=payload.period_end_date)
            leader_result = _combine_country_leader_forecasts(forecast, leader_chart, person.get("time_status", "unknown"), person.get("source", "reference database"))
        if leader_result:
            forecast["leader_country_synthesis"] = leader_result
        pdf=build_mundane_pdf({"analysis":analysis,"historical":history,"forecast":forecast})
        return Response(pdf,media_type="application/pdf",headers={"Content-Disposition":"attachment; filename=jyotish-lab-mundane-report.pdf"})
    except Exception as e:
        logger.exception("[PDF] Mundane PDF export failed"); raise HTTPException(status_code=500,detail="Mundane PDF export failed. Check server logs for details.")

@app.get("/api/mundane/backtest")
async def api_mundane_backtest(limit:int=Query(default=100, ge=1, le=500), window_days:int=Query(default=15, ge=1, le=365)):
    try:
        return retrospective_mundane_backtest(limit=limit, window_days=window_days)
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("[BACKTEST] Mundane backtest failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500,detail="Backtest failed. Check the request and server logs.")

@app.get("/api/mundane/historical-backtest")
async def api_mundane_historical_backtest(country: str = Query(min_length=1, max_length=120), start_date: str = Query(pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$"), end_date: str = Query(pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$"), horizon_days: int = Query(default=30, ge=1, le=365), step_days: int = Query(default=30, ge=14, le=365)):
    try:
        start = date.fromisoformat(start_date); end = date.fromisoformat(end_date)
        if end < start:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="end_date must be on or after start_date")
        if (end - start).days > 14610:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail="Historical backtest range cannot exceed 40 years")
        return retrospective_event_forecast_backtest(country, start_date, end_date, horizon_days, step_days)
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("[BACKTEST] Historical backtest failed")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Historical backtest failed. Check the request and server logs.")

"""Curated public-figure reference data loaded from the bundled JSON corpus.

Birth times are never invented. Profiles without a reliably verified public birth
time use a clearly labelled 12:00 local reference chart.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import json
from pathlib import Path

DATA_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data" / "reference_profiles.json"

FALLBACK_PEOPLE = [
    {"name":"Narendra Modi","category":"politician","dob":"1950-09-17","tob":None,"place":"Vadnagar, Gujarat, India","latitude":23.813,"longitude":72.638,"timezone":"Asia/Kolkata","time_status":"unknown","source":"PM India biography"},
    {"name":"Mahatma Gandhi","category":"historical_political","dob":"1869-10-02","tob":None,"place":"Porbandar, Gujarat, India","latitude":21.6417,"longitude":69.6293,"timezone":"Asia/Kolkata","time_status":"unknown","source":"Government of India / Rajghat chronology"},
]

def _load_people():
    try:
        rows=json.loads(DATA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if isinstance(rows,list) and rows:
            return rows
    except Exception:
        pass
    return FALLBACK_PEOPLE

PEOPLE = _load_people()

ALIASES={
    "modi":"Narendra Modi","narendra modi":"Narendra Modi","gandhi":"Mahatma Gandhi","mahatma gandhi":"Mahatma Gandhi",
    "rahul gandhi":"Rahul Gandhi","mamata":"Mamata Banerjee","mamata banerjee":"Mamata Banerjee","ambani":"Mukesh Ambani",
    "mukesh ambani":"Mukesh Ambani","elon musk":"Elon Musk","musk":"Elon Musk","bill gates":"Bill Gates","jeff bezos":"Jeff Bezos",
    "ratan tata":"Ratan Tata","dhirubhai ambani":"Dhirubhai Ambani",
}

def canonical_person(name:str)->str:
    k=" ".join(name.strip().lower().split())
    return ALIASES.get(k,name.strip())

def search_people(query:str="",category:str|None=None):
    q=query.strip().lower()
    rows=[p for p in PEOPLE if (not q or q in p.get("name","").lower()) and (not category or p.get("category")==category)]
    return rows

def get_person(name:str):
    c=canonical_person(name)
    for p in PEOPLE:
        if p.get("name")==c:return p
    raise KeyError(f"No reference profile for {name}")

"""Rule-transparent, personalized Vedic muhurta screening.

This is an electional-astrology rule screen, not an empirically validated outcome predictor.
All times are computed in the requested location/timezone with Swiss Ephemeris and Lahiri.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import date, datetime, time, timedelta, timezone
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
from typing import Any
import math
import swisseph as swe

from .engine import _time_to_jd, norm360, nakshatra_details, NAKSHATRAS

TITHI_NAMES = ["Pratipada","Dwitiya","Tritiya","Chaturthi","Panchami","Shashthi","Saptami","Ashtami","Navami","Dashami","Ekadashi","Dwadashi","Trayodashi","Chaturdashi","Purnima/Amavasya"]
YOGA_NAMES = ["Vishkambha","Priti","Ayushman","Saubhagya","Shobhana","Atiganda","Sukarma","Dhriti","Shula","Ganda","Vriddhi","Dhruva","Vyaghata","Harshana","Vajra","Siddhi","Vyatipata","Variyana","Parigha","Shiva","Siddha","Sadhya","Shubha","Shukla","Brahma","Indra","Vaidhriti"]
KARANA_NAMES = ["Bava","Balava","Kaulava","Taitila","Garaja","Vanija","Vishti (Bhadra)"]
WEEKDAYS = ["Monday","Tuesday","Wednesday","Thursday","Friday","Saturday","Sunday"]
RAHU_SEGMENT = {0:2,1:7,2:5,3:6,4:4,5:3,6:8} # Python weekday: Monday=0
YAMAGANDA_SEGMENT = {0:4,1:3,2:2,3:1,4:7,5:6,6:5}
GULIKA_SEGMENT = {0:6,1:5,2:4,3:3,4:2,5:1,6:7}

ACTIVITIES: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {
 "puja": {"label":"Specific puja / spiritual practice", "good_tithi":[1,2,3,5,7,10,11,12,13,15], "good_nak":[0,3,4,7,12,13,16,20,21,23,24,25,26], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[5,8,17,18]},
 "job_start": {"label":"Start a new job / joining", "good_tithi":[2,3,5,6,7,10,11,12,13], "good_nak":[3,4,7,12,13,16,20,21,22,25,26], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[5,8,17,18]},
 "property_buy": {"label":"Purchase property", "good_tithi":[2,3,5,6,7,10,11,12,13], "good_nak":[3,4,7,12,13,16,20,21,25,26], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[5,8,17,18]},
 "property_sell": {"label":"Sell property", "good_tithi":[2,3,5,6,7,10,11,12,13], "good_nak":[2,5,8,9,14,17,18,23], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[5,8,17,18]},
 "business_start": {"label":"Launch a business / open a shop", "good_tithi":[2,3,5,6,7,10,11,12,13], "good_nak":[3,4,7,12,13,16,20,21,22,25,26], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[5,8,17,18]},
 "expense": {"label":"Major expense / payment", "good_tithi":[2,3,5,6,10,11,12,13], "good_nak":[3,4,7,12,13,16,20,21,25,26], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[5,8,17,18]},
 "property_legal": {"label":"Property registration / legal signing", "good_tithi":[2,3,5,6,7,10,11,12,13], "good_nak":[3,4,7,12,13,16,20,21,25,26], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[5,8,17,18]},
 "travel": {"label":"Begin travel", "good_tithi":[2,3,5,7,10,11,13], "good_nak":[0,3,4,7,12,13,16,20,21,25,26], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[5,8,17,18]},
 "education": {"label":"Begin study / exam preparation", "good_tithi":[2,3,5,6,10,11,12,13], "good_nak":[3,4,5,12,13,16,20,21,24,25,26], "avoid_tithi":[4,8,9,14], "good_yoga":[1,2,3,4,5,7,8,11,12,14,16,18,20,21,22,23,24,25,26], "avoid_nak":[8,17,18,23]},
}

def _sidereal_sun_moon(jd: float) -> tuple[float,float]:
    swe.set_sid_mode(swe.SIDM_LAHIRI)
    flags=swe.FLG_SWIEPH|swe.FLG_SIDEREAL
    sun=swe.calc_ut(jd,swe.SUN,flags)[0][0]; moon=swe.calc_ut(jd,swe.MOON,flags)[0][0]
    return norm360(sun),norm360(moon)

def _panchanga(jd: float) -> dict[str,Any]:
    sun,moon=_sidereal_sun_moon(jd); elong=norm360(moon-sun); phase=int(elong//12)+1
    paksha="Shukla" if phase<=15 else "Krishna"; tithi_num=phase if phase<=15 else phase-15
    yoga_num=int(norm360(sun+moon)//(360/27))+1
    karana_idx=int(elong//6); karana = "Kimstughna" if karana_idx==0 else ("Shakuni" if karana_idx==57 else "Chatushpada" if karana_idx==58 else "Naga" if karana_idx==59 else KARANA_NAMES[(karana_idx-1)%7])
    nak=int(moon//(360/27)); nak_name=NAKSHATRAS[nak]
    return {"sun":sun,"moon":moon,"elongation":elong,"tithi_number":tithi_num,"tithi":TITHI_NAMES[tithi_num-1],"paksha":paksha,"yoga_number":yoga_num,"yoga":YOGA_NAMES[yoga_num-1],"karana":karana,"nakshatra_index":nak,"nakshatra":nak_name,"nakshatra_pada":int((moon%(360/27))/(360/108))+1}

def _sunrise_sunset(local_day: date, lat: float, lon: float, tzname: str) -> tuple[datetime|None,datetime|None]:
    tz=ZoneInfo(tzname); midnight=datetime.combine(local_day,time(0),tzinfo=tz); jd=_time_to_jd(midnight.astimezone(timezone.utc))
    try:
        geop=(lon,lat,0); rr,rt=swe.rise_trans(jd,swe.SUN,swe.CALC_RISE,geop); sr,st=swe.rise_trans(jd,swe.SUN,swe.CALC_SET,geop)
        rs=rt[0]; ss=st[0]
        if rr < 0 or sr < 0: return None,None
        def cv(x):
            y,m,d,ut=swe.revjul(x,swe.GREG_CAL); hh=int(ut); mm=int((ut-hh)*60); sec=int(round((((ut-hh)*60)-mm)*60)); return datetime(int(y),int(m),int(d),hh,mm,min(sec,59),tzinfo=timezone.utc).astimezone(tz)
        return cv(rs),cv(ss)
    except Exception: return None,None

def _segment_index(dt: datetime, sunrise: datetime|None, sunset: datetime|None) -> int|None:
    if not sunrise or not sunset or not (sunrise <= dt < sunset): return None
    part=(sunset-sunrise).total_seconds()/8
    return min(8, max(1, int((dt-sunrise).total_seconds()//part)+1))

def _clip_to_discrete_boundary(start: datetime, proposed_end: datetime, lat: float, lon: float, tzname: str, sunrise: datetime|None, sunset: datetime|None) -> datetime:
    """Clip a sample interval at the first Panchanga or daylight-segment change."""
    if proposed_end <= start: return proposed_end
    def signature(dt: datetime):
        p=_panchanga(_time_to_jd(dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)))
        return (p['tithi_number'],p['paksha'],p['nakshatra_index'],p['nakshatra_pada'],p['yoga_number'],p['karana'],_segment_index(dt,sunrise,sunset))
    initial=signature(start)
    if signature(proposed_end-timedelta(seconds=1)) == initial: return proposed_end
    lo=start; hi=proposed_end
    for _ in range(24):
        if (hi-lo).total_seconds() <= 1: break
        mid=lo+(hi-lo)/2
        if signature(mid)==initial: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    return hi

def _score_slot(dt: datetime, lat: float, lon: float, tzname: str, activity: str, natal_moon_lon: float|None, sunrise: datetime|None=None, sunset: datetime|None=None) -> dict[str,Any]:
    jd=_time_to_jd(dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)); p=_panchanga(jd); score=50; reasons=[]; penalties=[]; cfg=ACTIVITIES[activity]
    weekday=dt.weekday(); rahu_num=RAHU_SEGMENT[weekday]
    if sunrise and sunset and sunrise < dt < sunset:
        daylight=(sunset-sunrise).total_seconds()/8; seg=int((dt-sunrise).total_seconds()//daylight)+1
        if seg==rahu_num: score-=25; penalties.append("Rahu Kalam (avoid for auspicious initiation in this tradition)")
        else: reasons.append("Outside Rahu Kalam")
        if seg==YAMAGANDA_SEGMENT[weekday]: score-=8; penalties.append("Yamaganda segment")
        if seg==GULIKA_SEGMENT[weekday]: score-=5; penalties.append("Gulika segment")
    else:
        reasons.append("Night-time slot; daytime Rahu Kalam screen not applied")
    if p['tithi_number'] in cfg['good_tithi']: score+=10; reasons.append(f"Activity-compatible Tithi: {p['paksha']} {p['tithi']}")
    if p['tithi_number'] in cfg['avoid_tithi']: score-=12; penalties.append(f"Generally avoided Tithi for this activity: {p['tithi']}")
    if p['nakshatra_index'] in cfg['good_nak']: score+=12; reasons.append(f"Activity-compatible Nakshatra: {p['nakshatra']}")
    if p['nakshatra_index'] in cfg['avoid_nak']: score-=10; penalties.append(f"Traditionally avoided Nakshatra for this activity: {p['nakshatra']}")
    if p['yoga_number'] in cfg['good_yoga']: score+=7; reasons.append(f"Favourable general Nitya Yoga: {p['yoga']}")
    elif p['yoga_number'] in (6,9,10,13,15,17,19,27): score-=10; penalties.append(f"Traditionally inauspicious Nitya Yoga: {p['yoga']}")
    # Vara is a modest, explicitly secondary heuristic; school-specific rules vary.
    if activity in ('job_start','business_start','education') and weekday in (1,2,3,4): score+=3; reasons.append(f"Weekday support: {WEEKDAYS[weekday]}")
    if activity in ('property_buy','property_legal','puja') and weekday in (0,3,4): score+=3; reasons.append(f"Weekday support: {WEEKDAYS[weekday]}")
    if natal_moon_lon is not None:
        natal_nak=int(norm360(natal_moon_lon)//(360/27)); tara=((p['nakshatra_index']-natal_nak)%27)+1; tara_group=(tara-1)%9+1
        if tara_group in (1,3,5,7): score+=5; reasons.append(f"Tara Bala screen supportive (group {tara_group})")
        else: score-=5; penalties.append(f"Tara Bala screen challenging (group {tara_group})")
        moon_sign=int(p['moon']//30); natal_sign=int(norm360(natal_moon_lon)//30); chandra=((moon_sign-natal_sign)%12)+1
        if chandra in (1,3,6,7,10,11): score+=5; reasons.append(f"Chandra Bala screen supportive (Moon {chandra} signs from natal Moon)")
        else: score-=5; penalties.append(f"Chandra Bala screen challenging (Moon {chandra} signs from natal Moon)")
    score=max(0,min(100,score))
    return {"score":score,"panchanga":{k:v for k,v in p.items() if k not in ('sun','moon','elongation')},"reasons":reasons,"penalties":penalties,"sunrise":sunrise.isoformat() if sunrise else None,"sunset":sunset.isoformat() if sunset else None}

def search_muhurta(*, activity: str, start_date: str, end_date: str, latitude: float, longitude: float, timezone_name: str, natal_moon_longitude: float|None=None, step_minutes: int=30, max_results: int=20) -> dict[str,Any]:
    if activity not in ACTIVITIES: raise ValueError(f"Unsupported activity. Choose one of: {', '.join(ACTIVITIES)}")
    start=date.fromisoformat(start_date); end=date.fromisoformat(end_date)
    if end<start: raise ValueError("end_date must be on or after start_date")
    if (end-start).days>90: raise ValueError("Muhurta search is limited to 90 days per request; search longer periods in separate ranges")
    if not -90<=latitude<=90 or not -180<=longitude<=180: raise ValueError("Latitude/longitude out of range")
    tz=ZoneInfo(timezone_name); slots=[]; day=start
    while day<=end:
        sunrise,sunset=_sunrise_sunset(day,latitude,longitude,timezone_name)
        # Search daylight slots where sunrise/set are known, in local wall-clock time.
        if sunrise and sunset and sunset>sunrise:
            cur=sunrise
            while cur<sunset:
                proposed_end=min(cur+timedelta(minutes=step_minutes),sunset)
                slot_end=_clip_to_discrete_boundary(cur,proposed_end,latitude,longitude,timezone_name,sunrise,sunset)
                if slot_end<=cur: slot_end=min(cur+timedelta(seconds=1),sunset)
                result=_score_slot(cur,latitude,longitude,timezone_name,activity,natal_moon_longitude,sunrise,sunset)
                if result['score']>=60:
                    slots.append({"start":cur.isoformat(),"end":slot_end.isoformat(),**result})
                cur=slot_end
        day+=timedelta(days=1)
    # Merge adjacent eligible intervals only if score, Panchanga labels and rationale are identical.
    merged=[]
    for slot in slots:
        key=(slot['score'],slot['panchanga'],slot['reasons'],slot['penalties'])
        if merged and merged[-1]['end']==slot['start'] and (merged[-1]['score'],merged[-1]['panchanga'],merged[-1]['reasons'],merged[-1]['penalties'])==key:
            merged[-1]['end']=slot['end']
        else: merged.append(slot.copy())
    merged.sort(key=lambda x:(-x['score'],x['start']))
    # Keep top intervals, not multiple adjacent fragments; response warns that scores are rule screens.
    out=merged[:max_results]
    return {"activity":activity,"activity_label":ACTIVITIES[activity]['label'],"location":{"latitude":latitude,"longitude":longitude,"timezone":timezone_name},"period":{"start":start.isoformat(),"end":end.isoformat()},"method":{"ayanamsha":"Lahiri","ephemeris":"Swiss Ephemeris","time_basis":"local civil time; sunrise/sunset by location","rules":["Tithi","Nakshatra","Nitya Yoga","weekday (secondary)","Rahu Kalam/Yamaganda/Gulika daytime segments","Tara Bala and Chandra Bala when birth Moon is supplied"],"score_interpretation":"Rule-screen score (0–100), not a probability or validated outcome prediction."},"personalized":natal_moon_longitude is not None,"candidate_windows":out,"candidate_count_before_limit":len(merged),"limitations":["Traditions differ on activity-specific muhurta rules; this version exposes its selected rule screen.","Exact event-specific rules such as marriage, griha pravesha and regional panchanga exceptions are not silently assumed.","This score is not empirical evidence that an event will succeed; use practical, legal, financial and safety considerations too."]}


================================================================================
FILE: app/mundane.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Mapping
from pathlib import Path
import json
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

import swisseph as swe

from .engine import _calc_planets, angular_distance, sign_index, SIGNS, norm360


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MundaneEvent:
    country: str
    event_name: str
    local_date: str
    local_time: str
    place: str
    latitude: float
    longitude: float
    timezone: str
    event_type: str = "national"
    source_note: str = "Bundled reference event; verify the historical timestamp for research use."
    event_class: str | None = None

    @property
    def utc_dt(self) -> datetime:
        y, m, d = map(int, self.local_date.split("-"))
        parts = [int(x) for x in self.local_time.split(":")]
        h, mi = parts[:2]
        sec = parts[2] if len(parts) > 2 else 0
        return datetime(y, m, d, h, mi, sec, tzinfo=ZoneInfo(self.timezone)).astimezone(timezone.utc)


# Country chart corpus. The first/primary event is used automatically when a user
# supplies only a country name. Additional events remain available as historical
# manifestations of that country's chart signature.

# Expanded country chart corpus is kept as data so the mundane workspace can grow
# without coupling it to personal birth data.
_EXTRA_COUNTRY_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data" / "extra_country_charts.json"
try:
    _extra_country_rows = json.loads(_EXTRA_COUNTRY_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for _row in _extra_country_rows:
        REFERENCE_EVENTS.append(MundaneEvent(**_row))
except Exception:
    _extra_country_rows = []

# Historical events used specifically for out-of-sample / retrospective forecasting.
# They are kept separate from the canonical country-chart anchors so an event cannot
# accidentally become the country's primary birth chart.
_BACKTEST_EVENT_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / 'data' / 'mundane_backtest_events.json'
try:
    _backtest_rows = json.loads(_BACKTEST_EVENT_PATH.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    BACKTEST_EVENTS = [MundaneEvent(**{k:v for k,v in row.items() if k in MundaneEvent.__dataclass_fields__}) for row in _backtest_rows]
except Exception:
    _backtest_rows = []
    BACKTEST_EVENTS = []

    _mundane_corpus_entries = json.loads(_INTERPRETATION_CORPUS_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("entries", [])
except Exception:
    _mundane_corpus_entries = []

for _e in _mundane_corpus_entries:
    _MUNDANE_CORPUS_INDEX.setdefault((_e.get('type'),_e.get('key')),_e.get('text',''))

COUNTRY_ALIASES = {
    "usa": "United States", "us": "United States", "u.s.": "United States", "america": "United States",
    "uk": "United Kingdom", "britain": "United Kingdom", "england": "United Kingdom",
    "russia": "Russia", "russian federation": "Russia", "bharat": "India",
}


def canonical_country(name: str) -> str:
    key = " ".join(name.strip().lower().split())
    if key in COUNTRY_ALIASES:
        return COUNTRY_ALIASES[key]
    for event in REFERENCE_EVENTS:
        if event.country.lower() == key:
            return event.country
    return name.strip()


def available_countries() -> List[str]:
    return sorted({x.country for x in REFERENCE_EVENTS})


def resolve_country_chart(country: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    canonical = canonical_country(country)
    events = [e for e in REFERENCE_EVENTS if e.country == canonical]
    if not events:
        raise ValueError(f"No country chart is present in the corpus for '{country}'. Available countries: {', '.join(available_countries())}")
    # Prefer independence/founding, then constitution, then earliest national event.
    priority = {"independence": 0, "founding": 1, "constitution": 2, "constitutional": 3, "transition": 4}
    primary = sorted(events, key=lambda e: (priority.get(e.event_type, 9), e.local_date))[0]
    return {
        "country": canonical,
        "primary_chart": primary.__dict__,
        "alternative_charts": [e.__dict__ for e in events if e != primary],
        "chart_selection": "canonical corpus event: independence/founding preferred, then constitution, then transition/other",
    }


def _event_lagna(event: MundaneEvent) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    jd=swe.julday(event.utc_dt.year,event.utc_dt.month,event.utc_dt.day,
                  event.utc_dt.hour+event.utc_dt.minute/60+event.utc_dt.second/3600)
    try:
        _cusps, ascmc=swe.houses_ex(jd,event.latitude,event.longitude,b"W",swe.FLG_SIDEREAL)
        lon=norm360(ascmc[0])
    except Exception:
        lon=0.0
    return {'longitude':round(lon,6),'sign_index':sign_index(lon),'sign':SIGNS[sign_index(lon)]}

def _event_positions(event: MundaneEvent) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    jd = swe.julday(event.utc_dt.year, event.utc_dt.month, event.utc_dt.day,
                    event.utc_dt.hour + event.utc_dt.minute / 60 + event.utc_dt.second / 3600)
    return _calc_planets(jd)


def _signature(positions: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, Any]], lagna: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    planets = {p: {"sign": sign_index(v["longitude"]), "longitude": v["longitude"], "retrograde": v["retrograde"]}
               for p, v in positions.items()}
    lagna_sign = lagna.get('sign_index') if lagna else None
    if lagna_sign is not None:
        for p,v in planets.items():
            v['house_from_lagna']=((v['sign']-lagna_sign)%12)+1
    aspects: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    names = list(planets)
    for i, a in enumerate(names):
        for b in names[i + 1:]:
            d = angular_distance(planets[a]["longitude"], planets[b]["longitude"])
            nearest = min((0, 60, 90, 120, 180), key=lambda x: abs(d - x))
            orb = abs(d - nearest)
            if orb <= 4:
                aspects.append({"a": a, "b": b, "aspect": nearest, "orb": round(orb, 2)})
    return {"planets": planets, "aspects": aspects, "lagna": dict(lagna or {})}


def _signature_match(a: Mapping[str, Any], b: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    pa, pb = a["planets"], b["planets"]
    score = 0.0; max_score = 0.0; matches: List[str] = []
    # Country/event Lagna is explicitly part of the analogue signature.
    max_score += 6.0
    if a.get('lagna',{}).get('sign_index') == b.get('lagna',{}).get('sign_index'):
        score += 6.0
        matches.append(f"Lagna same sign ({a.get('lagna',{}).get('sign','')})")
    elif a.get('lagna',{}).get('sign_index') is not None and b.get('lagna',{}).get('sign_index') is not None:
        d=(b['lagna']['sign_index']-a['lagna']['sign_index'])%12
        if d in (4,8): score += 2.0; matches.append('Lagna trinal relation')
    for planet in ("Sun","Moon","Mars","Mercury","Jupiter","Venus","Saturn","Rahu","Ketu"):
        max_score += 2.0
        if pa.get(planet, {}).get("sign") == pb.get(planet, {}).get("sign"):
            score += 2.0; matches.append(f"{planet} same sign")
        elif pa.get(planet, {}).get("house_from_lagna") == pb.get(planet, {}).get("house_from_lagna"):
            score += 1.0; matches.append(f"{planet} same house from Lagna")
        elif pa.get(planet, {}).get("retrograde") == pb.get(planet, {}).get("retrograde"):
            score += 0.25
    aspect_pairs_a={(x['a'],x['b'],x['aspect']) for x in a['aspects']}; aspect_pairs_b={(x['a'],x['b'],x['aspect']) for x in b['aspects']}
    common=aspect_pairs_a & aspect_pairs_b; score += min(8.0,len(common)); max_score += 8.0
    if common: matches.extend([f"{x[0]}-{x[1]} {x[2]}°" for x in sorted(common)[:6]])
    return {"score":round(100*score/max_score,1),"matches":matches,"common_aspects":len(common),
            'lagna_match':a.get('lagna',{}).get('sign_index')==b.get('lagna',{}).get('sign_index')}


def _corpus_signatures(as_of_date: str | None = None) -> List[tuple[MundaneEvent, Dict[str, Any]]]:
    # Event signatures are invariant; compute each chart once and apply the as-of
    # cutoff only to the dated rows. This preserves no-future-leakage semantics
    # while avoiding recomputing every historical chart for every forecast anchor.
    all_rows = _CORPUS_SIGNATURE_CACHE.get("__all__")
    if all_rows is None:
        events = REFERENCE_EVENTS + BACKTEST_EVENTS + WORLD_EVENTS
        all_rows = [(event, _signature(_event_positions(event), _event_lagna(event))) for event in events]
        _CORPUS_SIGNATURE_CACHE["__all__"] = all_rows
    if not as_of_date:
        return all_rows
    return [row for row in all_rows if row[0].local_date <= as_of_date]


def analyze_country(country: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    resolved = resolve_country_chart(country)
    primary = MundaneEvent(**resolved["primary_chart"])
    positions = _event_positions(primary)
    lagna = _event_lagna(primary)
    return {
        "country": resolved["country"],
        "chart_source": resolved,
        "event": primary.__dict__,
        "positions": positions,
        "lagna": lagna,
        "signature": _signature(positions, lagna),
        "note": "The country chart is selected from the bundled national-event corpus. Historical similarity is pattern matching, not evidence that planetary configurations caused events.",
        "interpretation_corpus": {"mundane_rule_count": _MUNDANE_INTERPRETATION_RULE_COUNT, "mode": "deterministic historical-analogue interpretation"},
    }


def search_historical_patterns(country: str, limit: int = 30, event_types: Iterable[str] | None = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    analysis = analyze_country(country)
    target_sig = analysis["signature"]
    canonical = analysis["country"]
    type_filter = {x.strip().lower() for x in (event_types or []) if x.strip()}
    results = []
    for event, sig in _corpus_signatures():
        if event.country == canonical and event == MundaneEvent(**analysis["event"]):
            continue
        if type_filter and event.event_type.lower() not in type_filter:
            continue
        match = _signature_match(target_sig, sig)
        results.append({"event": event.__dict__, "same_country": event.country == canonical, **match})
    results.sort(key=lambda x: (-x["same_country"], -x["score"], x["event"]["local_date"]))
    return {
        "target_country": canonical,
        "target_chart": analysis["event"],
        "results": results[:max(1, min(limit, 200))],
        "corpus_size": len(REFERENCE_EVENTS) + len(WORLD_EVENTS),
        "method": "planet signs + planetary houses from Lagna + major aspects + Lagna relationship against dated historical event charts",
        "caveat": "Historical analogues are direct pattern references used by the deterministic mundane rule engine.",
    }

    'government':'Government and institutional power become the dominant national issue. Leadership changes, cabinet decisions, administrative restructuring and state authority are activated.',

def _mundane_domain_predictions(row):
    """Return a differentiated national-domain screen.

    Older versions started every domain at 50 and then added broad planet bonuses,
    which made nearly every domain appear "dominant". The revised screen uses a
    lower neutral baseline, domain-specific trigger bonuses, and an explicit
    prominence rank. All domains remain visible, but only the highest-ranked
    domains are called primary.
    """
    trigger_text=' '.join(row.get('triggers') or [])
    trigger_domains={
      'Jupiter':{'government':5,'economy':9,'markets':6,'foreign_relations':8,'infrastructure':5,'technology':4,'health_public':4,'agriculture_resources':6,'law_constitution':5,'public_mood':5,'international_trade':8,'institutions':6},
      'Saturn':{'government':7,'economy':6,'markets':4,'public_order':7,'conflict_defence':6,'infrastructure':9,'law_constitution':8,'institutions':10,'health_public':5,'agriculture_resources':5},
      'Rahu':{'markets':9,'public_order':7,'conflict_defence':6,'foreign_relations':8,'technology':11,'health_public':4,'public_mood':9,'international_trade':7,'government':5},
      'Ketu':{'public_order':5,'conflict_defence':5,'foreign_relations':4,'technology':5,'health_public':6,'agriculture_resources':4,'law_constitution':6,'institutions':6,'public_mood':7},
    }
    raw=[]
    for domain,weights in MUNDANE_DOMAINS.items():
        score=28.0
        for h,w in weights.items():
            if row.get('jupiter_house')==h: score += 13*w
            if row.get('saturn_house')==h: score += 9*w
            if row.get('rahu_house')==h: score += 5*w
            if row.get('ketu_house')==h: score += 4*w
        for planet,adds in trigger_domains.items():
            if planet in trigger_text: score += adds.get(domain,0)
        raw.append({'domain':domain,'score':max(0,min(100,round(score,1)))})
    raw.sort(key=lambda x:(-x['score'],x['domain']))
    out=[]
    for idx,item in enumerate(raw):
        domain=item['domain']; score=item['score']
        if idx < 3 and score >= 55:
            prominence='primary'
        elif idx < 8 and score >= 42:
            prominence='active'
        elif score >= 30:
            prominence='background'
        else:
            prominence='low'
        if prominence=='primary':
            text=MUNDANE_DIRECT[domain].replace('become the dominant national issue','is a primary national theme in this period')
        elif prominence=='active':
            text=f'{domain.replace("_"," ").title()} is an active secondary national theme. {MUNDANE_DIRECT[domain].split(". ",1)[-1]}'
        elif prominence=='background':
            text=f'{domain.replace("_"," ").title()} remains a background national theme; the current rule stack does not place it among the primary issues.'
        else:
            text=f'{domain.replace("_"," ").title()} has low activation in the current rule stack.'
        out.append({'domain':domain,'score':score,'strength':prominence,'prominence':prominence,'rank':idx+1,'prediction':text})
    return out


_MUNDANE_TRIGGER_RULES={
 'Jupiter': {0:'Jupiter conjunction: expansion, visibility, growth and institutional openings dominate the national chart.',60:'Jupiter sextile: constructive agreements, commerce, diplomacy and manageable expansion dominate.',90:'Jupiter square: growth collides with constraints; policy, finance and institutional capacity are stretched.',120:'Jupiter trine: expansion, alliances, education, trade and confidence receive strong support.',180:'Jupiter opposition: competing interests, expansion versus resistance and major negotiations dominate.'},
 'Saturn': {0:'Saturn conjunction: restructuring, accountability, institutional pressure and long-duration decisions dominate.',60:'Saturn sextile: disciplined reform, consolidation and practical institutional work dominate.',90:'Saturn square: delays, restrictions, fiscal or administrative pressure and structural correction dominate.',120:'Saturn trine: durable consolidation, institutional maturity and long-term implementation dominate.',180:'Saturn opposition: polarization between established structures and opposing interests becomes central.'},
 'Rahu': {0:'Rahu conjunction: amplification, disruption, technology, foreign influence and unconventional developments dominate.',60:'Rahu sextile: unconventional alliances, technology and rapid adaptation become prominent.',90:'Rahu square: volatility, competing narratives and sudden directional changes intensify.',120:'Rahu trine: rapid adoption, foreign links, technology and unconventional opportunities expand.',180:'Rahu opposition: polarized interests, abrupt reversals and competing external/internal pressures dominate.'},
 'Ketu': {0:'Ketu conjunction: detachment, separation, pruning, secrecy and abrupt simplification dominate.',60:'Ketu sextile: selective withdrawal, technical focus and simplification support reform.',90:'Ketu square: uncertainty, institutional discontinuity and loss of attachment to existing arrangements intensify.',120:'Ketu trine: simplification, technical correction and removal of obsolete structures dominate.',180:'Ketu opposition: a sharp divide develops between continuation and withdrawal, forcing choices.'},
}

def _mundane_blunt_interpretation(triggers, activation):
    if not triggers:
        return f'Activation {activation}: no slow-planet contact crosses the configured trigger threshold. This is a comparatively neutral period in the mundane rule set.'
    parts=[]
    for trig in triggers[:8]:
        planet,sep,rest=trig.partition(' '); aspect_s=rest.split('°',1)[0]
        try: aspect=int(aspect_s)
        except Exception: continue
        text=_MUNDANE_TRIGGER_RULES.get(planet,{}).get(aspect)
        if text: parts.append(text)
    return f'Activation {activation}: '+(' '.join(dict.fromkeys(parts)) if parts else 'multiple configured planetary contacts are active; the combined result is carried by the activation score and historical signature matches.')


def _mundane_deep_rules(domain, planet, house, aspect, event_types=None, pair=None):
    """Return stable, evidence-oriented mundane rules for a current signature.

    These rules never invent events. They explain which historical analogue
    dimensions should be compared, then the row supplies the dated events.
    """
    out=[]
    for rel in ('same_sign','trine_element','opposite_axis','same_modality'):
        key=f"{domain}|{planet}|H{house}|A{aspect}|{rel}"
        txt=_MUNDANE_CORPUS_INDEX.get(('mundane_lagna_analogue_rule',key))
        if txt: out.append(txt)
    if pair and event_types:
        a,b=pair
        for et in event_types[:4]:
            key=f"{a}|{b}|H{house}|A{aspect}|{et}"
            txt=_MUNDANE_CORPUS_INDEX.get(('mundane_pair_event_rule',key))
            if txt: out.append(txt)
    return list(dict.fromkeys(out))[:8]

# Event-specific validity filters. These are deliberately stricter than the
# generic planetary trigger screen: a dramatic event class is only surfaced when
# the signature contains the planets and national houses that actually belong to
# that event family. This was tightened using the Israel 2020-2026 and India
# 1970-1999 walk-forward backtests.
_EVENT_SIGNATURE_RULES = {
    'border_clash': {'trigger_planets': {'Mars','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu'}, 'natal_targets': {'Mars','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu','Jupiter'}, 'houses': {3,6,8,12}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 1, 'min_score': 62},
    'terror_attack': {'trigger_planets': {'Mars','Rahu','Ketu','Saturn'}, 'natal_targets': {'Mars','Rahu','Ketu','Sun','Moon'}, 'houses': {3,6,8,12}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 1, 'min_score': 68},
    'aviation_incident': {'trigger_planets': {'Rahu','Ketu','Mars','Saturn'}, 'natal_targets': {'Mercury','Moon','Mars'}, 'houses': {3,6,8,12}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 2, 'min_score': 70},
    'internal_unrest': {'trigger_planets': {'Mars','Rahu','Ketu','Saturn'}, 'natal_targets': {'Moon','Sun','Mars','Saturn','Rahu'}, 'houses': {1,3,6,8,10,11,12}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 2, 'min_score': 62},
    'public_uprising': {'trigger_planets': {'Mars','Rahu','Ketu','Saturn'}, 'natal_targets': {'Moon','Sun','Mars','Saturn','Rahu'}, 'houses': {1,4,6,8,11,12}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 2, 'min_score': 66},
    'government_fall': {'trigger_planets': {'Sun','Mars','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu'}, 'natal_targets': {'Sun','Saturn','Jupiter','Moon'}, 'houses': {1,8,10,11,12}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 2, 'min_score': 68},
    'executive_removal': {'trigger_planets': {'Sun','Mars','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu'}, 'natal_targets': {'Sun','Saturn','Jupiter','Moon'}, 'houses': {1,8,10,12}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 2, 'min_score': 68},
    'constitutional_crisis': {'trigger_planets': {'Saturn','Rahu','Ketu','Jupiter'}, 'natal_targets': {'Sun','Saturn','Jupiter','Mercury','Rahu'}, 'houses': {1,4,8,9,10,11}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 2, 'min_score': 64},
    'constitutional_crisis': {'trigger_planets': {'Saturn','Rahu','Ketu','Jupiter'}, 'natal_targets': {'Sun','Saturn','Jupiter','Mercury','Rahu'}, 'houses': {1,4,8,9,10,11}, 'aspects': {0,90,180}, 'min_distinct': 1, 'min_score': 62},
