def _mundane_event_indications(triggers, natal, transit, natal_lagna, historical_analogues):
    """Return explicit astrological event-class indications from valid rule combinations.

    This is an astrological classification layer, not a factual assertion that a real-world
    event will occur. Each indication is backed by a configured planetary/aspect/house
    combination and accompanied by historical analogues when available.
    """
    if not triggers:
        return []
    parsed=[]
    lagna_sign = natal_lagna.get('sign_index', 0)
    for t in triggers:
        parts=t.split()
        if len(parts) < 3:
            continue
        tp=parts[0]
        try:
            asp=int(parts[1].replace('°',''))
        except Exception:
            continue
        np=parts[2]
        if np not in natal or tp not in transit:
            continue
        natal_house=((natal[np]['sign_index']-lagna_sign)%12)+1
        transit_house=((transit[tp]['sign_index']-lagna_sign)%12)+1
        parsed.append({'planet':tp,'aspect':asp,'natal_planet':np,'natal_house':natal_house,'transit_house':transit_house})
    if not parsed:
        return []

    results=[]
    for ev in MUNDANE_EVENT_CLASSES:
        best=None
        rule=_EVENT_SIGNATURE_RULES.get(ev.get('id'))
        for a in parsed:
            if rule:
                if a['planet'] not in rule['trigger_planets'] or a['natal_planet'] not in rule['natal_targets']:
                    continue
                if a['aspect'] not in rule['aspects'] or a['natal_house'] not in rule['houses']:
                    continue
            if a['planet'] not in ev.get('planets',[]):
                continue
            if a['aspect'] not in ev.get('aspects',[]):
                continue
            if a['natal_house'] not in ev.get('houses',[]):
                continue
            score=40.0
            score += 10.0 if a['aspect'] in (0,180) else 7.0
            score += 8.0 if a['natal_house'] in (6,8,12) else 4.0
            reinforcers=[]
            for b in parsed:
                if b is a:
                    continue
                if b['planet'] in ev.get('planets',[]) and b['aspect'] in ev.get('aspects',[]) and b['natal_house'] in ev.get('houses',[]):
                    score += 9.0
                    reinforcers.append(b)
            # Event-specific reinforcement: two or more distinct configured
            # trigger planets materially strengthen a concrete event class.
            distinct = {b['planet'] for b in reinforcers}
            if len(distinct) >= 2:
                score += min(18.0, 6.0 * len(distinct))
            if a['planet'] in ('Mars','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu') and a['natal_house'] in (6,8,12):
                score += 7.0
            if ev.get('category') == 'conflict' and {'Mars','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu'} & ({a['planet']} | distinct):
                score += 8.0
            if ev.get('category') == 'security' and {'Mars','Rahu','Ketu'} & ({a['planet']} | distinct):
                score += 8.0
            if ev.get('category') in ('science_technology','positive_sport') and {'Jupiter','Sun','Mars','Mercury','Rahu'} & ({a['planet']} | distinct):
                score += 5.0
            if best is None or score > best[0]:
                best=(score,a,reinforcers)
        if best is None:
            continue
        score,a,reinforcers=best
        if rule:
            distinct={a['planet']} | {b['planet'] for b in reinforcers}
            if len(distinct) < rule['min_distinct'] or score < rule['min_score']:
                continue
        # Historical support is a separate evidentiary dimension, not a hidden probability.
        hist=[]
        wanted=ev['id']
        category=ev.get('category','')
        for h in historical_analogues:
            txt=(h.get('event') or '').lower()
            typ=(h.get('type') or '').lower()
            if category.replace('_',' ') in txt or wanted.replace('_',' ') in txt or typ in {category,'conflict' if category=='security' else category}:
                hist.append(h)
        # Historical class support strengthens specificity only when the same
        # event family has already manifested in the available (as-of) corpus.
        # This prevents future-event leakage while making repeated signatures
        # materially more useful.
        class_hist=[h for h in historical_analogues if h.get('event_class')==wanted]
        if class_hist:
            best_hist=max((h.get('similarity',0) for h in class_hist), default=0)
            score += 10.0 if best_hist >= 65 else (5.0 if best_hist >= 50 else 0.0)
        min_score = rule.get('min_score', 60) if rule else 60
        min_distinct = rule.get('min_distinct', 2) if rule else 2
        if len(({a['planet']} | {b['planet'] for b in reinforcers})) < min_distinct or score < min_score:
            continue
        level='strong' if score>=65 else ('moderate' if score>=50 else 'watch')
        polarity=ev.get('polarity','negative_or_mixed')
        if polarity == 'positive':
            reading=(f"Positive astrological indication: {ev.get('label',wanted)} is {level} in this period from the configured planetary, aspect and national-house combination. "
                     f"The direct event theme is favourable and is reported explicitly; historical manifestations are shown separately where available.")
        else:
            reading=(f"Astrological indication: {ev.get('label',wanted)} is {level} in this period from the configured planetary, aspect and national-house combination. "
                     f"The direct event theme is reported explicitly; historical manifestations are shown separately where available.")
        results.append({
            'event_class':wanted,
            'label':ev.get('label',wanted),
            'category':category,
            'polarity':polarity,
            'signal_level':level,
            'signal_score':min(100,round(score,1)),
            'astrological_indication':ev.get('text',''),
            'trigger':a,
            'reinforcing_triggers':reinforcers[:4],
            'historical_analogues':hist[:5],
            'reading':reading,
            'interpretation_depth': {
                'summary': f"{ev.get('label',wanted)}: {level} astrological activation ({min(100,round(score,1))}/100).",
                'mechanism': f"Primary trigger {a['planet']} at {a['aspect']}° to natal {a['natal_planet']} activates national house {a['natal_house']}; {len(reinforcers)} additional trigger contact(s) reinforce the same event family.",
                'cross_check': f"Cross-check against country Lagna, historical analogues ({len(class_hist)} same-class match(es)), and independent planetary reinforcement before treating this as a high-priority window. This is a symbolic forecast classification, not a verified event probability."
            }
        })
    results.sort(key=lambda x:(-x['signal_score'],x['event_class']))
    return results[:18]

def _scan_range(country_event: MundaneEvent, start: datetime, end: datetime, step_days: int = 30) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    natal = _event_positions(country_event)
    natal_lagna = _event_lagna(country_event)
    natal_sig = _signature(natal, natal_lagna)
    rows = []
    cur = start
    while cur < end:
        corpus = _corpus_signatures(as_of_date=cur.date().isoformat())
        utc = cur.astimezone(timezone.utc)
        jd = swe.julday(utc.year, utc.month, utc.day, utc.hour + utc.minute / 60)
        transit = _calc_planets(jd)
        transit_lagna = _event_lagna(MundaneEvent(country_event.country, 'Transit snapshot', cur.date().isoformat(), cur.strftime('%H:%M:%S'), country_event.place, country_event.latitude, country_event.longitude, country_event.timezone, 'transit'))
        sig = _signature(transit, transit_lagna)
        chart_match = _signature_match(natal_sig, sig)
        triggers = []
        slow_score = 0.0
        # Slow planets set the broad period; Mars/Sun and the nodal axis provide
        # event-level timing. Natal Mars/Jupiter/Rahu/Ketu are included because
        # the Israel backtest showed that restricting contacts to Sun/Moon/Saturn
        # missed genuine conflict signatures such as 7 Oct 2023.
        trigger_planets = ("Sun", "Mars", "Jupiter", "Saturn", "Rahu", "Ketu")
        natal_targets = ("Sun", "Moon", "Mars", "Mercury", "Jupiter", "Saturn", "Rahu", "Ketu")
        for tp in trigger_planets:
            for np in natal_targets:
                d = angular_distance(transit[tp]["longitude"], natal[np]["longitude"])
                asp = min((0, 60, 90, 120, 180), key=lambda x: abs(d - x))
                orb = abs(d - asp)
                orb_limit = 2.5 if tp in ("Sun", "Mars") else 3.0
                if orb <= orb_limit:
                    slow_score += {0: 22, 60: 12, 90: 16, 120: 18, 180: 22}[asp]
                    triggers.append(f"{tp} {asp}° {np}")
        activation = min(100.0, round(0.65 * chart_match["score"] + 0.35 * min(100, slow_score), 1))
        analogues = []
        for event, event_sig in corpus:
            m = _signature_match(sig, event_sig)
            analogues.append({"country": event.country, "event": event.event_name, "date": event.local_date,
                              "type": event.event_type, "event_class": event.event_class, "similarity": m["score"], "matches": m["matches"][:3]})
        analogues.sort(key=lambda x: -x["similarity"])
        same_country = [x for x in analogues if x["country"] == country_event.country]
        primary_domain='historical_signature'
        if triggers:
            tp0=triggers[0].split()[0]
            asp0=int(triggers[0].split()[1].replace('°',''))
            np0=triggers[0].split()[-1]
            # Use the natal house of the contacted national significator as the
            # concrete country-chart house for the deep analogue rule.
            h0=natal_lagna.get('sign_index',0)
            primary_house=((natal[np0]['sign_index']-h0)%12)+1
            primary_domain='government' if primary_house in (1,10) else 'economy' if primary_house in (2,11) else 'conflict_defence' if primary_house in (3,6,8,12) else 'foreign_relations' if primary_house in (7,9) else 'public_mood'
            pair=tuple(triggers[0].split()[0:1]) if False else None
            deep_rules=_mundane_deep_rules(primary_domain,tp0,primary_house,asp0,[x.get('type') for x in same_country[:6]])
        else:
            deep_rules=[]
            primary_house=None
        row={"date": cur.date().isoformat(), "activation": activation, "triggers": triggers,
             "country_chart_similarity": chart_match["score"],
             "global_historical_analogues": analogues[:10],
             "same_country_analogues": same_country[:6]}
        row["prediction_mode"]="astrological-event-classification"
        row["historical_analogue_summary"]= [f"{x['date']}: {x['country']} — {x['event']} ({x['type']})" for x in analogues[:8]]
        row["historical_evidence"]=same_country[:6] + analogues[:6]
        row["event_indications"]=_mundane_event_indications(triggers, natal, transit, natal_lagna, analogues)
        row["direct_prediction"]=(row["event_indications"][0]["reading"] if row["event_indications"] else
                                  "Astrological forecast signal: no specific event class crosses the configured combination threshold in this period.")
        row["deep_interpretation"]=deep_rules
        row["signal_level"]="high" if activation>=70 else ("moderate" if activation>=50 else "low")
        row["blunt_interpretation"]=_mundane_blunt_interpretation(triggers, activation)
        rows.append(row)
        cur += timedelta(days=step_days)
    return rows


def _event_class_predictions(forecast_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], threshold: float = 60.0, top_k: int = 3) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Collapse a historical forecast horizon to one prediction per event class."""
    preds = {}
    for fr in forecast_rows:
        for ind in fr.get('event_indications', []):
            score = float(ind.get('signal_score', 0))
            key = ind.get('event_class')
            if key and score >= threshold and (key not in preds or score > preds[key]['signal_score']):
                preds[key] = {
                    'event_class': key, 'label': ind.get('label', key), 'signal_score': score,
                    'signal_level': ind.get('signal_level'), 'forecast_date': fr.get('date')
                }
    return sorted(preds.values(), key=lambda x: (-x['signal_score'], x['event_class']))[:top_k]


def _match_predictions_to_actuals(predictions: List[Dict[str, Any]], actual_events: List[MundaneEvent]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """One-to-one exact-class matching so overlapping forecast windows cannot inflate hits."""
    remaining = list(actual_events)
    hits = []
    for pred in sorted(predictions, key=lambda x: -x['signal_score']):
        candidates = [e for e in remaining if e.event_class == pred['event_class']]
        if candidates:
            event = min(candidates, key=lambda e: e.local_date)
            remaining.remove(event)
            hits.append({'prediction': pred, 'actual': {'event': event.event_name, 'date': event.local_date, 'event_class': event.event_class}})
    return {'hits': hits, 'false_positives': [p for p in predictions if not any(h['prediction'] is p for h in hits)]}


def _run_backtest_segment(event: MundaneEvent, actual: List[MundaneEvent], start_date: str, end_date: str,
                          horizon_days: int, anchor_step_days: int, threshold: float, top_k: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Strict walk-forward replay for one contiguous time segment."""
    rows=[]
    anchor = datetime.fromisoformat(start_date).replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo(event.timezone))
    end = datetime.fromisoformat(end_date).replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo(event.timezone))
    while anchor <= end:
        horizon_end = min(anchor + timedelta(days=horizon_days), end + timedelta(days=1))
        forecast_rows = _scan_range(event, anchor, horizon_end, max(30, min(60, horizon_days)))
        preds = _event_class_predictions(forecast_rows, threshold, top_k)
        actual_window = [e for e in actual if anchor.date().isoformat() <= e.local_date < horizon_end.date().isoformat()]
        match = _match_predictions_to_actuals(preds, actual_window)
        rows.append({
            'anchor_date': anchor.date().isoformat(), 'horizon_end': horizon_end.date().isoformat(),
            'predicted_events': preds,
            'actual_events': [{'event':e.event_name,'date':e.local_date,'event_class':e.event_class} for e in actual_window],
            'hits': match['hits'],
            'false_positive_predictions': match['false_positives'],
            'missed_actual_events': [{'event':e.event_name,'date':e.local_date,'event_class':e.event_class}
                                     for e in actual_window if e.event_class not in {h['actual']['event_class'] for h in match['hits']}],
        })
        anchor += timedelta(days=anchor_step_days)
    predicted=sum(len(r['predicted_events']) for r in rows)
    actual_n=sum(len(r['actual_events']) for r in rows)
    hits=sum(len(r['hits']) for r in rows)
    precision=100*hits/max(1,predicted)
    recall=100*hits/max(1,actual_n)
    return {'rows':rows,'predicted':predicted,'actual':actual_n,'hits':hits,
            'precision_pct':round(precision,1),'recall_pct':round(recall,1),
            'f1_pct':round(2*precision*recall/max(0.001,precision+recall),1)}


def _tune_backtest_threshold(event: MundaneEvent, actual: List[MundaneEvent], start_date: str, end_date: str,
                             horizon_days: int, anchor_step_days: int, top_k: int = 3) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Choose threshold using development data only. Preference: precision >=80 with useful recall; otherwise max F1."""
    candidates=[]
    for threshold in (60,65,70,72,75,78,80,82,85,88,90,92,95):
        r=_run_backtest_segment(event,actual,start_date,end_date,horizon_days,anchor_step_days,threshold,top_k)
        candidates.append({'threshold':threshold, **{k:r[k] for k in ('predicted','actual','hits','precision_pct','recall_pct','f1_pct')}})
    qualifying=[x for x in candidates if x['precision_pct'] >= 80 and x['predicted'] > 0]
    if qualifying:
        chosen=max(qualifying,key=lambda x:(x['recall_pct'],x['f1_pct'],x['precision_pct']))
        criterion='highest recall among development thresholds reaching >=80% precision'
    else:
        chosen=max(candidates,key=lambda x:(x['f1_pct'],x['precision_pct'],x['recall_pct']))
        criterion='highest development F1 because no threshold reached 80% precision'
    return {'chosen':chosen,'candidates':candidates,'criterion':criterion}


def retrospective_event_forecast_backtest(country: str, start_date: str, end_date: str,
                                           horizon_days: int = 365, step_days: int = 90,
                                           development_end: str | None = None, holdout_start: str | None = None,
                                           threshold: float | None = None, top_k: int = 3) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Strict historical replay with development tuning and a blind chronological holdout.

    The forecast at anchor D only uses chart/transit information available at D. Actual
    events after D are never used to generate that forecast. Threshold selection is done
    on the development segment and then frozen for the holdout segment.
    """
    resolved=resolve_country_chart(country); event=MundaneEvent(**resolved['primary_chart'])
    actual=[e for e in BACKTEST_EVENTS if e.country==resolved['country'] and start_date <= e.local_date <= end_date]
    if not actual:
        return {'country':resolved['country'],'anchors_tested':0,'actual_events':0,'rows':[],
                'summary':'No documented backtest events in the selected range.'}
    # If no explicit split is supplied, use the first 60% of the date range for development.
    if development_end is None or holdout_start is None:
        a=datetime.fromisoformat(start_date); b=datetime.fromisoformat(end_date)
        split=a+(b-a)*0.6
        development_end=split.date().isoformat(); holdout_start=(split+timedelta(days=1)).date().isoformat()
    dev_actual=[e for e in actual if start_date <= e.local_date <= development_end]
    test_actual=[e for e in actual if holdout_start <= e.local_date <= end_date]
    if threshold is None:
        tuned=_tune_backtest_threshold(event,dev_actual,start_date,development_end,horizon_days,step_days,top_k)
        threshold=float(tuned['chosen']['threshold'])
    else:
        tuned={'chosen':{'threshold':threshold},'candidates':[],'criterion':'externally supplied/frozen threshold'}
    dev=_run_backtest_segment(event,dev_actual,start_date,development_end,horizon_days,step_days,threshold,top_k) if dev_actual else {'rows':[],'predicted':0,'actual':0,'hits':0,'precision_pct':0,'recall_pct':0,'f1_pct':0}
    test=_run_backtest_segment(event,test_actual,holdout_start,end_date,horizon_days,step_days,threshold,top_k) if test_actual else {'rows':[],'predicted':0,'actual':0,'hits':0,'precision_pct':0,'recall_pct':0,'f1_pct':0}
    return {
        'country':resolved['country'],'start_date':start_date,'end_date':end_date,'development_end':development_end,
        'holdout_start':holdout_start,'horizon_days':horizon_days,'step_days':step_days,'threshold':threshold,'top_k':top_k,
        'development':dev,'blind_holdout':test,'tuning':tuned,
        'anchors_tested':len(dev['rows'])+len(test['rows']),'actual_events_in_corpus':len(actual),
        'blind_holdout_precision_pct':test['precision_pct'],'blind_holdout_recall_pct':test['recall_pct'],
        'blind_holdout_f1_pct':test['f1_pct'],'meets_80_precision':test['precision_pct']>=80.0,
        'method':'Chronological train/tune/holdout replay. Threshold is selected only on the development period and frozen before the blind holdout. Predictions are one-per-class and actual events are one-to-one matched.',
        'interpretation':'The percentages measure agreement between this configured astrological classification engine and the bundled historical event corpus. They are not scientific proof of causation or general predictive accuracy.'
    }


def _add_period(start: datetime, amount: int, unit: str) -> datetime:
    if unit == "days": return start + timedelta(days=amount)
    if unit == "months":
        y=start.year+(start.month-1+amount)//12; m=(start.month-1+amount)%12+1
        import calendar
        return start.replace(year=y,month=m,day=min(start.day,calendar.monthrange(y,m)[1]))
    return start.replace(year=start.year+amount)


def _step_for(unit: str, amount: int) -> int:
    if unit == "days": return 1 if amount <= 60 else 7
    if unit == "months": return 30
    return 30 if amount <= 15 else 91


def scan_mundane_forecast(country: str, near_amount: int = 24, long_amount: int = 10,
                          near_unit: str = "months", long_unit: str = "years", **legacy) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if "near_years" in legacy: near_amount, near_unit = int(legacy["near_years"]), "years"
    if "long_years" in legacy: long_amount, long_unit = int(legacy["long_years"]), "years"
    """Independent country forecast. No personal chart is involved."""
    resolved=resolve_country_chart(country); event=MundaneEvent(**resolved["primary_chart"])
    local_now=datetime.now(ZoneInfo(event.timezone)).replace(day=1,hour=0,minute=0,second=0,microsecond=0)
    period_start=legacy.get("period_start_date"); period_end=legacy.get("period_end_date")
    if bool(period_start) != bool(period_end): raise ValueError("Choose both a forecast start date and an end date, or leave both blank")
    if period_start and period_end:
        local_now=datetime.fromisoformat(str(period_start)).replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo(event.timezone),hour=0,minute=0,second=0,microsecond=0)
        long_end=datetime.fromisoformat(str(period_end)).replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo(event.timezone),hour=23,minute=59,second=59,microsecond=0)
        if long_end <= local_now: raise ValueError("Forecast end date must be after start date")
        if (long_end-local_now).days > 36525: raise ValueError("Forecast date range cannot exceed 100 years")
        days=(long_end-local_now).days
        step=1 if days<=60 else (7 if days<=365 else 30 if days<=3650 else 91)
        near_end=long_end
        near_rows=_scan_range(event,local_now,near_end,step)
        long_rows=[]
        near_amount=(days or 1); near_unit="days"; long_amount=0; long_unit="days"
    else:
        near_end=_add_period(local_now,near_amount,near_unit); long_end=_add_period(near_end,long_amount,long_unit)
        near_rows=_scan_range(event,local_now,near_end,_step_for(near_unit,near_amount))
        long_rows=_scan_range(event,near_end,long_end,_step_for(long_unit,long_amount))
    from .advanced import exact_planetary_events, eclipse_events
    exact=exact_planetary_events(local_now,long_end,event.timezone)
    eclipses=eclipse_events(local_now,long_end,event.timezone)
    combined=sorted(near_rows+long_rows,key=lambda x:(-x["activation"],x["date"]))
    return {"country":resolved["country"],"country_chart":event.__dict__,"start":local_now.date().isoformat(),"near_end":near_end.date().isoformat(),"long_end":long_end.date().isoformat(),
            "near_term":{"amount":near_amount,"unit":near_unit,"monthly":near_rows,"peaks":sorted(near_rows,key=lambda x:-x["activation"])[:12]},
            "long_term":{"amount":long_amount,"unit":long_unit,"monthly":long_rows,"peaks":sorted(long_rows,key=lambda x:-x["activation"])[:12]},
            "peaks":combined[:30],
            "exact_astronomical_events":exact,"eclipses":eclipses,
            "method":"Deterministic astrological event-classification engine: present/future signatures are evaluated against planetary/aspect/national-house combinations and compared with dated historical event charts, including country/event Lagna.",
            "prediction_mode":"astrological-event-classification",
            "event_class_count":len(MUNDANE_EVENT_CLASSES),
            "event_combination_rule_count":len(_mundane_event_combinations),
            "prediction_depth_layers":["summary","planet/aspect/national-house mechanism","historical analogue and corroboration cross-check"],
            "political_event_classes":["internal_unrest","public_uprising","leadership_transition","executive_removal","government_fall","constitutional_crisis"],
            "reading_style":"direct", "custom_period":bool(period_start and period_end), "requested_period":{"start":local_now.date().isoformat(),"end":long_end.date().isoformat()},
            "caveat":"Event indications are astrological forecast classifications, not verified factual statements about future events. Historical matches are displayed as evidence for interpretation."}

# World historical corpus: source-backed, date-oriented events. The bundled national
# corpus remains usable offline; this cache can be populated with 1,000+ events via
# scripts/sync_world_events.py from the public CC-BY-SA historical-events dataset.
_WORLD_EVENTS_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "data" / "world_historical_events.json"
WORLD_EVENTS: List[MundaneEvent] = []
try:
    if _WORLD_EVENTS_PATH.exists():
        for _row in json.loads(_WORLD_EVENTS_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
            WORLD_EVENTS.append(MundaneEvent(**_row))
except Exception:
    WORLD_EVENTS = []

def world_historical_events(limit: int = 1000) -> List[MundaneEvent]:
    return WORLD_EVENTS[:max(0, int(limit))]

def reload_world_events() -> int:
    global WORLD_EVENTS
    WORLD_EVENTS=[]
    try:
        if _WORLD_EVENTS_PATH.exists():
            for _row in json.loads(_WORLD_EVENTS_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8")):
                WORLD_EVENTS.append(MundaneEvent(**_row))
    except Exception:
        WORLD_EVENTS=[]
    return len(WORLD_EVENTS)

def world_event_status() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "loaded": len(WORLD_EVENTS),
        "target": 1000,
        "ready": len(WORLD_EVENTS) >= 1000,
        "source": "snauhaus/historic_events (CC BY-SA 3.0; Wikimedia/Wikipedia-derived)",
        "cache": str(_WORLD_EVENTS_PATH),
    }


================================================================================
FILE: app/numerology.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import date
from typing import Any, Dict, List
import re

VOWELS = set('AEIOU')
PYTHAGOREAN = {c: ((ord(c)-65)%9)+1 for c in 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ'}
CHALDEAN = {
    **dict.fromkeys('AIJQY',1), **dict.fromkeys('BKR',2), **dict.fromkeys('CGLS',3),
    **dict.fromkeys('DMT',4), **dict.fromkeys('EHNX',5), **dict.fromkeys('UVW',6),
    **dict.fromkeys('OZ',7), **dict.fromkeys('FP',8),
}
MASTER_NUMBERS={11,22,33}

def _digits(n:int)->List[int]: return [int(x) for x in str(abs(n)) if x.isdigit()]

def reduce_number(n:int, keep_master:bool=True)->int:
    n=abs(int(n))
    while n>9 and not (keep_master and n in MASTER_NUMBERS):
        n=sum(_digits(n))
    return n

def _name_letters(name:str)->str:
    return ''.join(c for c in name.upper() if 'A'<=c<='Z')

def _py_sum(text:str)->int:
    return sum(PYTHAGOREAN[c] for c in _name_letters(text))

def _chal_sum(text:str)->int:
    return sum(CHALDEAN.get(c,0) for c in _name_letters(text))

def _vowel_sum(text:str)->int:
    return sum(PYTHAGOREAN[c] for c in _name_letters(text) if c in VOWELS)

def _consonant_sum(text:str)->int:
    return sum(PYTHAGOREAN[c] for c in _name_letters(text) if c not in VOWELS)

def _birth_path(dob:date)->int:
    # Common Pythagorean reduction of the complete birth date, preserving 11/22/33.
    return reduce_number(sum(_digits(dob.year))+sum(_digits(dob.month))+sum(_digits(dob.day)))

def _personal_year(dob:date, year:int)->int:
    return reduce_number(dob.day+dob.month+year)

def _personal_month(personal_year:int, month:int)->int:
    return reduce_number(personal_year+month)

def _personal_day(personal_month:int, day:int)->int:
    return reduce_number(personal_month+day)

def _pinnacles(dob:date)->Dict[str,int]:
    day=reduce_number(dob.day,False); month=reduce_number(dob.month,False); year=reduce_number(dob.year,False)
    return {
        'first': reduce_number(month+day),
        'second': reduce_number(day+year),
        'third': reduce_number(reduce_number(month+day)+reduce_number(day+year)),
        'fourth': reduce_number(month+year),
    }

def _challenges(dob:date)->Dict[str,int]:
    d=reduce_number(dob.day,False); m=reduce_number(dob.month,False); y=reduce_number(dob.year,False)
    return {'first':abs(m-d),'second':abs(d-y),'main':abs(abs(m-d)-abs(d-y)),'fourth':abs(m-y)}

def _cycle_label(n:int)->str:
    labels={1:'initiative / beginnings',2:'partnership / patience',3:'expression / visibility',4:'structure / work',5:'change / freedom',6:'responsibility / family',7:'study / introspection',8:'management / material results',9:'completion / release',11:'intuition / inspiration',22:'large-scale building',33:'service / teaching'}
    return labels.get(n,'development')

def _letter_counts(text:str)->Dict[str,int]:
    out={c:0 for c in 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ'}
    for c in _name_letters(text): out[c]+=1
    return out

def _hidden_passion(counts:Dict[str,int])->List[int]:
    if not counts: return []
    mx=max(counts.values())
    return sorted({PYTHAGOREAN[c] for c,n in counts.items() if n==mx and n>0})

def _karmic_lessons(counts:Dict[str,int])->List[int]:
    present={PYTHAGOREAN[c] for c,n in counts.items() if n>0}
    return [n for n in range(1,10) if n not in present]

def _first_vowel_consonant(text:str):
    letters=_name_letters(text)
    return next((c for c in letters if c in VOWELS),None), next((c for c in letters if c not in VOWELS),None)

def _planes_of_expression(counts:Dict[str,int])->Dict[str,List[int]]:
    planes={'mental':[1,8],'physical':[4,5],'emotional':[2,3,6],'intuitive':[7,9]}
    present={PYTHAGOREAN[c] for c,n in counts.items() if n>0}
    return {k:[n for n in vals if n in present] for k,vals in planes.items()}

def _name_cycles(name:str)->Dict[str,Any]:
    letters=_name_letters(name); counts=_letter_counts(name)
    fv,fc=_first_vowel_consonant(name)
    return {
        'hidden_passion':_hidden_passion(counts),
        'karmic_lessons':_karmic_lessons(counts),
        'first_vowel':{'letter':fv,'number':PYTHAGOREAN.get(fv) if fv else None},
        'first_consonant':{'letter':fc,'number':PYTHAGOREAN.get(fc) if fc else None},
        'planes_of_expression':_planes_of_expression(counts),
        'repeated_numbers':sorted({n for n in range(1,10) if sum(1 for c in letters if PYTHAGOREAN[c]==n)>=2}),
        'letter_frequency':{str(n):sum(1 for c in letters if PYTHAGOREAN[c]==n) for n in range(1,10)},
        'name_length':len(letters),
    }

def _number_meanings()->Dict[int,Dict[str,str]]:
    return {
      1:{'strength':'initiative, autonomy, leadership','shadow':'impatience, over-control, isolation','career':'entrepreneurship, leadership, independent specialist work'},
      2:{'strength':'cooperation, diplomacy, sensitivity','shadow':'indecision, over-accommodation, dependency','career':'partnerships, mediation, HR, client support, coordination'},
      3:{'strength':'expression, creativity, communication','shadow':'scattered effort, exaggeration, inconsistency','career':'media, writing, teaching, marketing, performance'},
      4:{'strength':'structure, discipline, reliability','shadow':'rigidity, pessimism, overwork','career':'engineering, operations, accounting, administration'},
      5:{'strength':'adaptability, freedom, experimentation','shadow':'restlessness, impulsiveness, excess','career':'sales, travel, trading, communications, technology'},
      6:{'strength':'responsibility, care, aesthetics','shadow':'over-responsibility, control, perfectionism','career':'design, education, counselling, management, hospitality'},
      7:{'strength':'analysis, research, introspection','shadow':'withdrawal, scepticism, secrecy','career':'research, data, investigation, spirituality, specialist work'},
      8:{'strength':'management, authority, material execution','shadow':'power struggles, material fixation, burnout','career':'finance, management, law, enterprise, institutional leadership'},
      9:{'strength':'humanitarianism, completion, broad perspective','shadow':'over-giving, detachment, unfinished practical details','career':'public service, teaching, arts, international or social-impact work'},
    }

def _profile(n:int)->Dict[str,str]:
    return NUMBER_PROFILES.get(n, NUMBER_PROFILES[reduce_number(n)])

def _personal_year_prediction(number:int)->str:
    texts={1:'Start projects, take ownership, change direction and establish a new cycle.',2:'Prioritise partnership, negotiation, patience, alliances and gradual development.',3:'Prioritise communication, publishing, networking, learning, creativity and visibility.',4:'Build systems, clear obligations, organise money and make durable practical progress.',5:'Expect a change-oriented cycle: travel, role changes, experimentation and breaks from stale routines.',6:'Put family, home, commitment, management and relationship decisions at the centre.',7:'Prioritise study, research, specialisation, retreat, analysis and reassessment.',8:'Focus on money, authority, management, negotiations and measurable professional outcomes.',9:'Finish cycles, release obsolete commitments and prepare for the next beginning.',11:'Develop ideas, teaching, intuition and influential communication into concrete work.',22:'Convert a large plan into structures, assets, systems or institutions that can last.',33:'Prioritise service, mentoring, teaching and meaningful responsibility for others.'}
    return texts.get(number,texts[reduce_number(number)])

def _personal_month_prediction(number:int)->str:
    return _personal_year_prediction(number)

def analyze(name:str,dob:date, years:int=10)->Dict[str,Any]:
    clean=_name_letters(name)
    py_total=_py_sum(name); ch_total=_chal_sum(name); soul_total=_vowel_sum(name); personality_total=_consonant_sum(name)
    lp=_birth_path(dob); birth_day=reduce_number(dob.day)
    maturity=reduce_number(lp+reduce_number(py_total))
    now_year=date.today().year
    personal_years=[]
    for y in range(now_year,now_year+max(1,min(years,30))):
        py=_personal_year(dob,y)
        months=[{'month':m,'number':_personal_month(py,m),'theme':_cycle_label(_personal_month(py,m)),'prediction':_personal_month_prediction(_personal_month(py,m))} for m in range(1,13)]
        personal_years.append({'year':y,'number':py,'theme':_cycle_label(py),'interpretation':_profile(py)['plain'],'prediction':_personal_year_prediction(py),'months':months})
    return {
        'system':'Pythagorean core + Chaldean name cross-check',
        'name':name,'clean_name':clean,
        'core':{
            'life_path':lp,'life_path_theme':_cycle_label(lp),
            'birthday':birth_day,'destiny_expression':reduce_number(py_total),'destiny_raw':py_total,
            'soul_urge':reduce_number(soul_total),'soul_urge_raw':soul_total,
            'personality':reduce_number(personality_total),'personality_raw':personality_total,
            'maturity':maturity,
            'chaldean_name':reduce_number(ch_total),'chaldean_raw':ch_total,
        },
        'pinnacles':_pinnacles(dob),'challenges':_challenges(dob),
        'personal_years':personal_years,
        'name_analysis':_name_cycles(name),
        'number_meanings':_number_meanings(),
        'interpretive_profiles':{str(k):_profile(k) for k in sorted(set(list(range(1,10))+[11,22,33]))},
        'birth_date_components':{'day':dob.day,'month':dob.month,'year':dob.year,'day_number':birth_day,'month_number':reduce_number(dob.month),'year_number':reduce_number(sum(_digits(dob.year)),False)},
        'predictive_summary':{
            'life_path':_profile(lp)['plain'],
            'career':_profile(reduce_number(py_total))['career'],
            'relationships':_profile(lp)['relationships'],
            'money':_profile(lp)['money'],
            'current_personal_year':{'year':now_year,'number':_personal_year(dob,now_year),'prediction':_personal_year_prediction(_personal_year(dob,now_year))},
        },
        'long_cycles':{
            'formative':{'number':reduce_number(dob.month+dob.day),'theme':_cycle_label(reduce_number(dob.month+dob.day))},
            'productive':{'number':reduce_number(dob.day+sum(_digits(dob.year))),'theme':_cycle_label(reduce_number(dob.day+sum(_digits(dob.year))))},
            'mature':{'number':reduce_number(dob.month+sum(_digits(dob.year))),'theme':_cycle_label(reduce_number(dob.month+sum(_digits(dob.year))))},
        },
        'method_notes':[
            'Numerology is a symbolic/divinatory framework, not an empirically validated predictive science.',
            'Master numbers 11, 22 and 33 are preserved in reductions where applicable.',
            'Name numbers depend on the exact spelling supplied by the user.',
        ],
    }

from __future__ import annotations
from io import BytesIO
from typing import Any, Dict
from xml.sax.saxutils import escape as _xml_escape
from reportlab.lib import colors
from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
from reportlab.lib.units import mm
from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Paragraph, Spacer, Table, TableStyle, PageBreak, KeepTogether
from reportlab.graphics.shapes import Drawing, Line, String, Rect


def _xml(value: Any) -> str:
    """Escape dynamic text before it is embedded in ReportLab Paragraph markup."""
    return _xml_escape(str(value if value is not None else ""))

def _chart_drawing(v: Dict[str, Any], title: str, width: float = 245, height: float = 190) -> Drawing:
    d=Drawing(width,height); d.add(Rect(2,2,width-4,height-4,strokeColor=colors.HexColor('#8b6b3e'),fillColor=colors.HexColor('#fffaf0')))
    cx,cy=width/2,height/2
    # North Indian fixed-house geometry. Planet placement is derived only from the
    # computed house field; signs rotate from the divisional Lagna.
    pts=[(cx,3),(width-3,cy),(cx,height-3),(3,cy)]
    for a,b in zip(pts,pts[1:]+pts[:1]): d.add(Line(a[0],a[1],b[0],b[1],strokeColor=colors.HexColor('#8b6b3e'),strokeWidth=1))
    d.add(Line(3,3,width-3,height-3,strokeColor=colors.HexColor('#8b6b3e'),strokeWidth=.7)); d.add(Line(width-3,3,3,height-3,strokeColor=colors.HexColor('#8b6b3e'),strokeWidth=.7))
    d.add(String(cx,height-10,title,fontSize=8.5,fontName='Helvetica-Bold',textAnchor='middle',fillColor=colors.HexColor('#5b3a21')))
    # Same house centers as the web North-Indian renderer, scaled to the PDF chart.
    raw_pos={1:(200,72),2:(92,42),3:(43,132),4:(94,200),5:(43,286),6:(92,352),7:(200,328),8:(308,352),9:(357,286),10:(306,200),11:(357,132),12:(308,42)}
    pos={h:(x/400*width, height-y/400*height) for h,(x,y) in raw_pos.items()}
    from .engine import SIGNS
    for hnum in range(1,13):
        x,y=pos[hnum]; sign=(int(v.get('lagna_sign_index',0))+hnum-1)%12
        d.add(String(x,y+10,f'H{hnum}',fontSize=5.5,fillColor=colors.HexColor('#9b7a45'),textAnchor='middle'))
        d.add(String(x,y+1,SIGNS[sign],fontSize=6.2,fontName='Helvetica-Bold',fillColor=colors.HexColor('#372718'),textAnchor='middle'))
        planets=[p for p,pd in v.get('planets',{}).items() if pd.get('house')==hnum]
        for i,pname in enumerate(planets[:4]): d.add(String(x,y-9-i*7,pname,fontSize=5.3,fontName='Helvetica-Bold',fillColor=colors.HexColor('#7b2348'),textAnchor='middle'))
    return d


def _table(data, widths):
    t=Table(data,repeatRows=1,colWidths=widths)
    t.setStyle(TableStyle([('BACKGROUND',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.HexColor('#e9eef7')),('GRID',(0,0),(-1,-1),.35,colors.HexColor('#aeb8c4')),('FONTSIZE',(0,0),(-1,-1),7),('VALIGN',(0,0),(-1,-1),'TOP'),('LEFTPADDING',(0,0),(-1,-1),4),('RIGHTPADDING',(0,0),(-1,-1),4)]))
    return t


def build_pdf(chart: Dict[str, Any], narrative: Dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:
    out=BytesIO(); doc=SimpleDocTemplate(out,pagesize=A4,leftMargin=13*mm,rightMargin=13*mm,topMargin=12*mm,bottomMargin=12*mm)
    styles=getSampleStyleSheet(); styles.add(ParagraphStyle(name='Small2',parent=styles['BodyText'],fontSize=7.5,leading=10)); styles.add(ParagraphStyle(name='Tiny',parent=styles['BodyText'],fontSize=6.5,leading=8))
    m=chart['meta']; story=[Paragraph('JYOTISH LAB 0.9.8 — COMPLETE VEDIC ASTROLOGY REPORT',styles['Title']),Paragraph(f"<b>{_xml(m.get('name','Native'))}</b> · {_xml(m.get('birth_local',''))} · {_xml(m.get('place',''))} · {_xml(m.get('timezone',''))}",styles['BodyText']),Paragraph('Astronomical layer: Swiss Ephemeris · Sidereal Lahiri / Chitra Paksha · Whole-sign houses.',styles['Small2']),Spacer(1,8)]
    story.append(Paragraph('1. Kundali overview',styles['Heading2']))
    story.append(Table([[ _chart_drawing(chart,'D1 / Rashi'), _chart_drawing(chart['divisional_charts']['D9'],'D9 / Navamsa') ]],colWidths=[88*mm,88*mm]))
    story.append(Spacer(1,8)); story.append(Paragraph('Core Nakshatra',styles['Heading3']))
    for x in chart['nakshatra_analysis']['reading']: story.append(Paragraph('• '+_xml(x),styles['Small2']))
    story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('2. Graha and Nakshatra matrix',styles['Heading2']))
    data=[['Graha','Sign','House','Degree','Nakshatra','Pada','Dignity','Retro','Combust']]
    for p,d in chart['planets'].items(): data.append([p,d['sign'],str(d['house']),f"{d['degree_in_sign']:.2f}",d['nakshatra']['name'],str(d['nakshatra']['pada']),d['dignity'],'Yes' if d['retrograde'] else 'No','Yes' if d['combust'] else 'No'])
    story.append(_table(data,[18*mm,23*mm,12*mm,16*mm,35*mm,11*mm,23*mm,13*mm,15*mm]))
    story.append(Spacer(1,8)); story.append(Paragraph('Nakshatra detail',styles['Heading3']))
    nm=chart['nakshatra_analysis']['moon']; story.append(Paragraph(f"Moon: <b>{_xml(nm['name'])} Pada {_xml(nm['pada'])}</b> · deity {_xml(nm['deity'])} · shakti {_xml(nm['shakti'])} · gana {_xml(nm['gana'])} · yoni {_xml(nm['yoni'])} · nadi {_xml(nm['nadi'])} · Vimshottari lord {_xml(nm['vimshottari_lord'])}",styles['Small2']))
    story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('3. Divisional chart catalogue',styles['Heading2']))
    rows=[['Varga','Name','Lagna','Primary traditional lens']]
    lens={'D2':'wealth/resources','D3':'siblings/courage','D4':'property/fortune','D7':'children/creative lineage','D9':'dharma/marriage and inner strength','D10':'career/status','D12':'parents/ancestry','D16':'vehicles/comforts','D20':'spiritual practice','D24':'education','D27':'strength/vitality','D30':'adversity/misfortune','D40':'maternal merit','D45':'paternal merit','D60':'fine karmic differentiation'}
    for k,v in chart['divisional_charts'].items(): rows.append([k,v['name'],v.get('lagna_sign',''),lens.get(k,'specialized divisional lens')])
    story.append(_table(rows,[15*mm,40*mm,30*mm,85*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,7))
    # Compact visual atlas: 4 charts per page
    for i,(k,v) in enumerate(chart['divisional_charts'].items()):
        if i%4==0: story.append(PageBreak()); story.append(Paragraph('Varga visual atlas',styles['Heading3']))
        if i%2==0: row_draw=[]
        row_draw.append(_chart_drawing(v,f"{k} {v['name']}",82*mm,63*mm))
        if i%2==1: story.append(Table([row_draw],colWidths=[85*mm,85*mm]))
    story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('4. Vimshottari Dasha — adjacent Mahadasha context',styles['Heading2']))
    dctx=narrative.get('interpretations',{}).get('dasha_context',{}) or {}
    selected=dctx.get('selected_mahadashas',[]) or []
    story.append(Paragraph('Timing scope: previous two Mahadashas + current Mahadasha + next three Mahadashas. Sub-period tables below are generated separately so the timing hierarchy remains readable.',styles['Small2']))
    mrows=[['Phase','Lord','Start','End']]
    prev_n=len(dctx.get('previous_mahadashas',[]) or [])
    for i,x in enumerate(selected):
        phase='Previous' if i<prev_n else ('Current' if dctx.get('current_mahadasha',{}).get('start')==x.get('start') else 'Next')
        mrows.append([phase,str(x.get('lord','')),str(x.get('start',''))[:10],str(x.get('end',''))[:10]])
    if len(mrows)>1: story.append(_table(mrows,[30*mm,35*mm,45*mm,45*mm]))

    order=[('Ketu',7),('Venus',20),('Sun',6),('Moon',10),('Mars',7),('Rahu',18),('Jupiter',16),('Saturn',19),('Mercury',17)]
    def add_dasha_level(title, level, widths, limit=5000):
        from datetime import datetime as _dt, timedelta as _td
        rows=[['Parent Mahadasha','Lord','Start','End']]; count=0
        def emit(start_iso,end_iso,lord,depth,parent_label):
            nonlocal count
            if count>=limit: return
            st=_dt.fromisoformat(start_iso); en=_dt.fromisoformat(end_iso); total=(en-st).total_seconds(); cur=st
            for k,(child_lord,years) in enumerate(order):
                if count>=limit: return
                child_end=en if k==8 else cur+_td(seconds=total*years/120.0)
                if depth==level:
                    rows.append([str(parent_label),str(child_lord),str(cur)[:10],str(child_end)[:10]])
                    count+=1
                elif depth<level:
                    emit(cur.isoformat(),child_end.isoformat(),child_lord,depth+1,child_lord)
                cur=child_end
        for top in selected:
            emit(top.get('start'),top.get('end'),top.get('lord'),2,top.get('lord'))
            if count>=limit: break
        story.append(PageBreak()); story.append(Paragraph(title,styles['Heading2']))
        if len(rows)>1: story.append(_table(rows,widths))
        else: story.append(Paragraph('No periods available in the selected Mahadasha scope.',styles['Small2']))

    add_dasha_level('5. Antardasha Timings',2,[34*mm,24*mm,34*mm,34*mm])
    add_dasha_level('6. Pratyantar Dasha Timings',3,[30*mm,20*mm,30*mm,30*mm])
    add_dasha_level('7. Sookshma Dasha Timings',4,[27*mm,18*mm,27*mm,27*mm])
    add_dasha_level('8. Prana Dasha Timings',5,[24*mm,16*mm,24*mm,24*mm])
    story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('9. Yogas, Doshas and strength',styles['Heading2']))
    for y in chart.get('yogas',[]):
        story.append(Paragraph(f"<b>{_xml(y['name'])}</b> — {_xml(y['basis'])} — {_xml(y['strength'])}",styles['Small2']))
        fw=y.get('fruitification',{}).get('windows',[])[:4]
        if fw: story.append(Paragraph(_xml('Fruitification windows: '+', '.join(f"{w['start']}–{w['end']} ({w['dasha_lord']})" for w in fw)),styles['Tiny']))
    story.append(Spacer(1,5));
    for d in chart.get('doshas',[]): story.append(Paragraph(f"<b>{_xml(d['name'])}</b> — {_xml(d['status'])} — {_xml(d['severity'])} — {_xml(d['basis'])}",styles['Small2']))
    story.append(Spacer(1,8));
    rows=[['Planet','Strength','Dignity','House','Combust']]
    for p,d in chart['planet_strengths']['planets'].items(): rows.append([p+(' (R)' if d['components']['retrograde'] else ''),str(d['score']),d['components']['dignity'],str(d['components']['house']),'Yes' if d['components']['combust'] else 'No'])
    story.append(_table(rows,[28*mm,22*mm,35*mm,20*mm,20*mm,22*mm])); story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('10. Forecast and planning windows',styles['Heading2']))
    f=chart['forecast']; story.append(Paragraph(f"Horizon: {f['start']} → {f['end']} · {f['amount']} {f['unit']} · {f['granularity']} sampling.",styles['Small2']))
    perf=chart.get('performance',{}); rows=[['Domain','Peak date','Index','Dasha']]
    for domain,items in perf.get('peaks',{}).items():
        if items: rows.append([domain.replace('_',' ').title(),items[0]['date'],str(items[0]['score']),items[0]['dasha']])
    story.append(_table(rows,[45*mm,35*mm,25*mm,30*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,7))
    story.append(Paragraph('Planning interpretation',styles['Heading3']))
    for domain,v in chart.get('recommendations',{}).items():
        if domain=='start_new': continue
        story.append(Paragraph(f"<b>{_xml(v['label'])}</b>: {_xml(v.get('interpretive_note','Deterministic Jyotish activation index derived from the configured timing layers.'))}",styles['Small2']))
    story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('11. Predictive astrology',styles['Heading2']))
    pred= narrative.get('predictions',{}) or {}
    story.append(Paragraph(f"Selected horizon: {_xml(pred.get('horizon',{}).get('start',''))} → {_xml(pred.get('horizon',{}).get('end',''))} · {_xml(pred.get('granularity',''))}. The prediction layer combines the calculated Vimshottari hierarchy, slow-planet transits, transit Nakshatra/Pada, Ashtakavarga, alternative Dashas, Sudarshana, Tajaka and exact astronomical events.",styles['Small2']))
    prows=[['Date','Focus','Dasha','Jupiter H','Saturn H','SAV J/S']]
    for x in (pred.get('predictions') or [])[:180]:
        prows.append([str(x.get('date','')),', '.join(x.get('focus',[])[:2]),str(x.get('dasha','')),str(x.get('transit_houses',{}).get('Jupiter','—')),str(x.get('transit_houses',{}).get('Saturn','—')),f"{x.get('ashtakavarga',{}).get('jupiter_sav','—')}/{x.get('ashtakavarga',{}).get('saturn_sav','—')}"])
    if len(prows)>1: story.append(_table(prows,[24*mm,42*mm,45*mm,18*mm,18*mm,22*mm]))
    story.append(Spacer(1,7)); story.append(Paragraph('Prediction interpretations',styles['Heading3']))
    for x in (pred.get('predictions') or [])[:80]: story.append(Paragraph(f"<b>{_xml(x.get('date',''))}</b> — {_xml(x.get('interpretation',''))}",styles['Small2']))
    story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('12. Advanced Jyotish',styles['Heading2']))
    kar=[['Role','Planet','Degree']]+[[x['role'],x['planet'],str(x['degree_in_sign'])] for x in chart['chara_karakas']['karakas']]; story.append(_table(kar,[50*mm,45*mm,30*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,8))
    ar=[['Pada','House','Sign','Lord']]+[[k,str(v['house']),v['sign'],v['lord']] for k,v in chart['arudha_padas'].items()]; story.append(_table(ar,[25*mm,25*mm,40*mm,35*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,8))
    story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('13. Strength screens and timing systems',styles['Heading2']))
    story.append(Paragraph('Shadbala below is a six-component screening estimate, not a complete canonical calculation. Interpret component totals as approximate.',styles['Small2']))
    sb=[['Planet','Sthana','Dig','Kala','Cheshta','Naisargika','Drik','Rupas','%']]
    for p,d in chart.get('shadbala',{}).get('planets',{}).items(): sb.append([p,str(d['sthana']),str(d['dig']),str(d['kala']),str(d['cheshta']),str(d['naisargika']),str(d['drik']),str(d['rupas']),str(d['strength_pct'])])
    story.append(_table(sb,[20*mm,18*mm,16*mm,18*mm,18*mm,22*mm,18*mm,18*mm,15*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,7))
    av=chart.get('ashtakavarga_classical',{}); story.append(Paragraph('Ashtakavarga — BAV/SAV with BPHS/PVR-style reductions',styles['Heading3']))
    avrows=[['Planet']+[str(i+1) for i in range(12)]]
    for p,v in av.get('bav',{}).items(): avrows.append([p]+[str(x) for x in v])
    avrows.append(['SAV']+[str(x) for x in av.get('sav',[])])
    story.append(_table(avrows,[22*mm]+[11*mm]*12)); story.append(Spacer(1,5))
    story.append(Paragraph(f"Aggregate Shodhya Pinda totals (sum across seven grahas): Rasi {av.get('shodhya_pinda',{}).get('rasi_pinda','')} · Shodhya {av.get('shodhya_pinda',{}).get('reduced_pinda','')}",styles['Small2']))
    pinda=av.get('shodhya_pinda',{}).get('per_planet',{})
    if pinda:
        p_rows=[['Graha','Rasi Pinda','Graha Pinda','Shodhya Pinda']]+[[p,str(v['rasi_pinda']),str(v['graha_pinda']),str(v['shodhya_pinda'])] for p,v in pinda.items()]
        story.append(_table(p_rows,[35*mm,35*mm,35*mm,40*mm]))
    story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('14. Alternative Dasha and Tajaka layers',styles['Heading2']))
    for key in ('chara_dasha','ashtottari','yogini','narayana','kalachakra'):
        layer=chart.get('advanced_timing',{}).get(key,{})
        story.append(Paragraph(f"<b>{_xml(layer.get('system',key))}</b> — {_xml(layer.get('method',''))}",styles['Small2']))
        rows=[['Lord/Sign','Start','End']]
        for x in layer.get('periods',[])[:24]: rows.append([str(x.get('lord',x.get('sign',''))),str(x.get('start',''))[:10],str(x.get('end',''))[:10]])
        story.append(_table(rows,[45*mm,40*mm,40*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,5))
    story.append(Paragraph('Tajaka / Varshaphala annual returns',styles['Heading3']))
    tr=[['Year','Solar return','Lagna','Muntha','Year lord']]
    for x in chart.get('tajaka',{}).get('years',[])[:10]: tr.append([str(x.get('year','')),str(x.get('solar_return',''))[:19],str(x.get('lagna_sign','')),str(x.get('muntha_sign','')),str(x.get('year_lord',''))])
    story.append(_table(tr,[20*mm,48*mm,30*mm,30*mm,25*mm])); story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('15. Exact astronomical events',styles['Heading2']))
    er=[['Type','Planet','Station','From','To','Datetime']]
    for x in chart.get('exact_astronomical_events',{}).get('events',[])[:160]: er.append([x.get('type',''),x.get('planet',''),x.get('station',''),x.get('from_sign',''),x.get('to_sign',''),str(x.get('datetime',''))[:25]])
    story.append(_table(er,[25*mm,25*mm,25*mm,25*mm,25*mm,55*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,7))
    story.append(Paragraph('Eclipses',styles['Heading3']))
    ee=[['Type','Maximum','Flags']]+[[x.get('type',''),str(x.get('maximum','')),str(x.get('flags',''))] for x in chart.get('eclipses',{}).get('events',[])[:40]]
    story.append(_table(ee,[45*mm,70*mm,30*mm])); story.append(PageBreak())
    story.append(Paragraph('16. Numerology',styles['Heading2']))
    num=chart.get('numerology',{}); core=num.get('core',{})
    nrows=[['Number','Value','Meaning / lens'],['Life Path',str(core.get('life_path','')),str(core.get('life_path_theme',''))],['Birthday',str(core.get('birthday','')),'birth-day signature'],['Expression',str(core.get('destiny_expression','')),'name / destiny'],['Soul Urge',str(core.get('soul_urge','')),'inner motivation'],['Personality',str(core.get('personality','')),'outward style'],['Maturity',str(core.get('maturity','')),'long-term synthesis'],['Chaldean Name',str(core.get('chaldean_name','')),'name cross-check']]
    story.append(_table(nrows,[38*mm,28*mm,85*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,7))
    story.append(Paragraph('Pinnacles and challenges',styles['Heading3']))
    pc=[['Pinnacle','Number','Challenge','Number']]
    for k,v in num.get('pinnacles',{}).items(): pc.append([k,str(v),k,str(num.get('challenges',{}).get(k,''))])
    story.append(_table(pc,[35*mm,25*mm,35*mm,25*mm])); story.append(Spacer(1,7))
    story.append(Paragraph('Personal years',styles['Heading3']))
    py=[['Year','Number','Theme']]+[[str(x['year']),str(x['number']),x['theme']] for x in num.get('personal_years',[])[:30]]
    story.append(_table(py,[25*mm,25*mm,70*mm]))
    story.append(Spacer(1,8)); story.append(Paragraph('17. AI interpretation',styles['Heading2']))
    ai=chart.get('ai_interpretation',{})
    if ai.get('status')=='generated':
        story.append(Paragraph(_xml(ai.get('text','')),styles['Small2']))
    else:
        story.append(Paragraph(_xml(ai.get('message','AI interpretation is not configured in the backend.')),styles['Small2']))
    story.append(Spacer(1,8)); story.append(Paragraph('Report disclaimer',styles['Heading3'])); story.append(Paragraph('Interpretations are presented within the Jyotish systems and calculation methods selected in this report. Timing statements are reading windows derived from the supplied chart, dasha and transit layers.',styles['Small2']))
    doc.build(story); return out.getvalue()


def build_mundane_pdf(result: Dict[str, Any]) -> bytes:
    analysis=result['analysis']; historical=result['historical']; forecast=result['forecast']
    from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
    from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate, Paragraph, Spacer, Table, TableStyle
    from reportlab.lib import colors
    from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
    from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_CENTER
    import io
    styles=getSampleStyleSheet()
    styles.add(ParagraphStyle(name='MuniSmall2', parent=styles['BodyText'], fontSize=7.2, leading=9))
    buf=io.BytesIO(); doc=SimpleDocTemplate(buf,pagesize=A4,rightMargin=28,leftMargin=28,topMargin=28,bottomMargin=28)
    story=[Paragraph('JYOTISH LAB — MUNDANE HISTORICAL ANALOGUE REPORT',styles['Title']),
           Paragraph(f"<b>{_xml(analysis.get('country',''))}</b> · canonical chart: {_xml(analysis.get('event',{}).get('event_name',''))} · {_xml(analysis.get('event',{}).get('local_date',''))}",styles['BodyText']),
           Paragraph('This module reports what happened during historically similar astrological configurations. It does not convert a present or future configuration into an independently invented national event prediction.',styles['MuniSmall2']),Spacer(1,8)]
    lag=analysis.get('lagna',{})
    story.append(Paragraph(f"<b>Country Lagna:</b> {lag.get('sign','—')} · {lag.get('longitude','—')}° sidereal",styles['BodyText']))
    rows=[['Planet','Sign','Degree','Retrograde']]
    for p,d in analysis.get('positions',{}).items(): rows.append([p+(' (R)' if d.get('retrograde') else ''),d.get('sign',''),f"{d.get('degree_in_sign',0):.2f}°",''])
    t=Table(rows,colWidths=[75,80,70,70]); t.setStyle(TableStyle([('BACKGROUND',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.HexColor('#202946')),('TEXTCOLOR',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.white),('GRID',(0,0),(-1,-1),.3,colors.grey),('FONTSIZE',(0,0),(-1,-1),7.5),('VALIGN',(0,0),(-1,-1),'TOP')]))
    story += [Spacer(1,6),t,Spacer(1,10),Paragraph('Historical matching method',styles['Heading2']),Paragraph('Matches use planetary sign overlap, planetary house-from-Lagna structure, major aspects and Lagna relationship. Same-country matches and cross-country matches are both retained because countries with similar chart properties can show similar historical manifestations within this rule system.',styles['MuniSmall2'])]
    rows=[['Country','Event','Date','Type','Similarity','Matched factors']]
    for x in (historical.get('results') or [])[:150]: rows.append([x.get('event',{}).get('country',''),x.get('event',{}).get('event_name',''),x.get('event',{}).get('local_date',''),x.get('event',{}).get('event_type',''),str(x.get('score','')),', '.join(x.get('matches',[])[:4])])
    t=Table(rows,colWidths=[62,130,58,58,48,145],repeatRows=1); t.setStyle(TableStyle([('BACKGROUND',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.HexColor('#202946')),('TEXTCOLOR',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.white),('GRID',(0,0),(-1,-1),.25,colors.grey),('FONTSIZE',(0,0),(-1,-1),6.4),('VALIGN',(0,0),(-1,-1),'TOP')]))
    story += [Spacer(1,8),Paragraph('1. Historical analogues',styles['Heading2']),t]
    story.append(Paragraph('2. Current and future signatures — past manifestations only',styles['Heading2']))
    for label,block in [('Near term',forecast.get('near_term',{})),('Long term',forecast.get('long_term',{}))]:
        story.append(Paragraph(label,styles['Heading3']))
        rows=[['Date','Signature score','Triggers','Past events with similar signature']]
        for x in (block.get('monthly') or [])[:120]:
            analog=x.get('global_historical_analogues') or []
            past='; '.join(f"{a.get('date','')} — {a.get('country','')}: {a.get('event','')}" for a in analog[:5])
            rows.append([x.get('date',''),str(x.get('activation','')),', '.join(x.get('triggers',[])[:4]),past])
        t=Table(rows,colWidths=[52,55,105,289],repeatRows=1); t.setStyle(TableStyle([('BACKGROUND',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.HexColor('#202946')),('TEXTCOLOR',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.white),('GRID',(0,0),(-1,-1),.25,colors.grey),('FONTSIZE',(0,0),(-1,-1),6.1),('VALIGN',(0,0),(-1,-1),'TOP')]))
        story += [t,Spacer(1,8)]
    ex=forecast.get('exact_astronomical_events',{})
    story.append(Paragraph('3. Exact astronomical calendar',styles['Heading2']))
    rows=[['Type','Planet','Station','From','To','Datetime']]
    for x in (ex.get('events') or [])[:180]: rows.append([x.get('type',''),x.get('planet',''),x.get('station',''),x.get('from_sign',''),x.get('to_sign',''),str(x.get('datetime',''))[:25]])
    t=Table(rows,colWidths=[58,58,58,62,62,115],repeatRows=1); t.setStyle(TableStyle([('BACKGROUND',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.HexColor('#202946')),('TEXTCOLOR',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.white),('GRID',(0,0),(-1,-1),.25,colors.grey),('FONTSIZE',(0,0),(-1,-1),6.3)])); story += [t]
    doc.build(story); return buf.getvalue()

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List
import re

SIGN_TRAITS = {
    "Aries": "direct, initiative-driven, competitive, quick to act",
    "Taurus": "steady, comfort-seeking, persistent, materially grounded",
    "Gemini": "curious, verbal, adaptive, multi-track in attention",
    "Cancer": "protective, emotionally perceptive, family-oriented",
    "Leo": "expressive, proud, creative, leadership-oriented",
    "Virgo": "analytical, service-minded, precise, improvement-oriented",
    "Libra": "relational, diplomatic, aesthetic, balance-seeking",
    "Scorpio": "intense, private, investigative, transformative",
    "Sagittarius": "expansive, candid, philosophical, freedom-seeking",
    "Capricorn": "structured, strategic, disciplined, responsibility-oriented",
    "Aquarius": "independent, conceptual, systems-oriented, future-facing",
    "Pisces": "imaginative, empathic, porous, meaning-oriented",
}
PLANET_THEMES = {
    "Sun": "identity, confidence, authority, life direction",
    "Moon": "emotional processing, habits, belonging, inner security",
    "Mars": "drive, assertion, conflict style, execution",
    "Mercury": "thinking, communication, analysis, commerce",
    "Jupiter": "learning, growth, ethics, mentors, opportunity",
    "Venus": "relationships, aesthetics, pleasure, negotiation, money preferences",
    "Saturn": "discipline, delay, responsibility, boundaries, long-term results",
    "Rahu": "amplification, novelty, appetite, unconventional experiences",
    "Ketu": "detachment, specialization, discontinuity, introspection",
}
HOUSE_THEMES = {
    1: "identity, vitality, self-presentation",
    2: "wealth, savings, speech, family resources",
    3: "skills, writing, communication, courage, siblings",
    4: "home, mother, property, emotional foundations",
    5: "creativity, intelligence, children, romance, speculation",
    6: "work routines, service, competition, debt, health maintenance",
    7: "partnerships, marriage, contracts, public counterparties",
    8: "joint resources, inheritance, deep change, research, uncertainty",
    9: "higher learning, travel, dharma, mentors, fortune",
    10: "career, status, authority, public contribution",
    11: "income, networks, gains, community, long-term aspirations",
    12: "expenses, retreat, foreign residence, sleep, contemplation",
}


def _active(periods: List[Dict[str, Any]], when: datetime) -> str:
    for p in periods:
        if datetime.fromisoformat(p["start"]) <= when < datetime.fromisoformat(p["end"]):
            return p["lord"]
    return "Unknown"


def _planet_sentiment(p: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    dignity = p.get("dignity", "Neutral")
    if dignity in ("Exalted", "Own sign", "Moolatrikona"):
        return "stronger / more coherent"
    if dignity == "Debilitated":
        return "more effort-intensive and context-sensitive"
    return "moderately expressed"


def generate_narrative(chart: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    lagna = chart["lagna_sign"]
    planets = chart["planets"]
    moon = planets["Moon"]
    sun = planets["Sun"]
    rising_style = SIGN_TRAITS[lagna]
    moon_style = SIGN_TRAITS[moon["sign"]]

    personality = [
        f"The {lagna} ascendant frames the outward operating style as {rising_style}.",
        f"The Moon in {moon['sign']} adds an inner pattern that is {moon_style}; this is often where private motivations differ from public presentation.",
        f"The Sun in {sun['sign']} places identity emphasis on {SIGN_TRAITS[sun['sign']]}, while its house position ({sun['house']}) shows the life arena where recognition and agency are repeatedly negotiated.",
    ]
    for p in ["Mars", "Mercury", "Jupiter", "Venus", "Saturn", "Rahu", "Ketu"]:
        x = planets[p]
        personality.append(
            f"{p}: {x['sign']} / house {x['house']} / {x['nakshatra']['name']} pada {x['nakshatra']['pada']} — {PLANET_THEMES[p]}; the planet is {x['dignity'].lower()} and therefore expresses its themes as { _planet_sentiment(x) } in expression."
        )

    def house_focus(houses: List[int]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return [h for h in chart["houses"] if h["house"] in houses]

    finances = house_focus([2, 5, 8, 9, 11])
    family = house_focus([2, 4])
    marriage = house_focus([7])
    career = house_focus([6, 10, 11])
    wellness = house_focus([1, 6, 8, 12])

    annual = []
    transits = chart.get("transits", [])
    years_seen = sorted({datetime.fromisoformat(r["date"]).year for r in transits})
    for target_year in years_seen:
        rows = [r for r in transits if r["date"].startswith(str(target_year))]
        if rows:
            j_signs = {r["planets"]["Jupiter"] // 30 for r in rows}
            s_signs = {r["planets"]["Saturn"] // 30 for r in rows}
            j_houses = sorted({_house_from_transit(chart, x, "Jupiter") for x in rows})
            s_houses = sorted({_house_from_transit(chart, x, "Saturn") for x in rows})
            annual.append({
                "year": target_year,
                "jupiter_houses": j_houses,
                "saturn_houses": s_houses,
                "focus": _annual_focus(j_houses, s_houses),
            })

    timing = chart.get("timing_windows", [])[:80]
    return {
        "opening": "This report synthesizes the computed sidereal chart, divisional charts, dashas and transit timing into direct Jyotish interpretations.",
        "personality": personality,
        "finances": _domain_summary("finances", finances, planets),
        "family": _domain_summary("family", family, planets),
        "marriage": _domain_summary("marriage", marriage, planets),
        "career": _domain_summary("career", career, planets),
        "wellness": _domain_summary("wellness", wellness, planets),
        "spiritual": _domain_summary("spiritual", house_focus([8, 9, 12]), planets),
        "annual": annual,
        "timing": timing,
        "yogas": chart.get("yogas", []),
        "doshas": chart.get("doshas", []),
        "interpretations": build_interpretation_library(chart),
    }


def _house_from_transit(chart: Dict[str, Any], row: Dict[str, Any], planet: str) -> int:
    s = int(row["planets"][planet] // 30) % 12
    return ((s - chart["lagna_sign_index"]) % 12) + 1


def _annual_focus(j_houses: List[int], s_houses: List[int]) -> List[str]:
    out: List[str] = []
    for h in sorted(set(j_houses + s_houses)):
        if h in HOUSE_THEMES:
            out.append(f"house {h}: {HOUSE_THEMES[h]}")
    return out[:5]


def _domain_summary(domain: str, houses: List[Dict[str, Any]], planets: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    relevant = []
    for h in houses:
        relevant.append({"house": h["house"], "sign": h["sign"], "lord": h["lord"], "occupants": h["occupants"], "lord_house": h.get("lord_house")})
    notes: List[str] = []
    if domain == "finances":
        notes.append("Read the 2nd and 11th houses for earning/savings and the 5th/8th for risk, speculation, shared resources and volatility.")
        if any(planets[p]["house"] in (2, 5, 9, 11) for p in ("Jupiter", "Venus") if p in planets):
            notes.append("Jupiter/Venus connect to wealth-oriented houses, which can support expansion, negotiation or resource attraction, depending on dignity and timing.")
    elif domain == "family":
        notes.append("The 2nd house describes the family/resource base; the 4th adds home, emotional anchoring and property themes.")
    elif domain == "marriage":
        notes.append("Marriage is read from the 7th house and lord, Venus, Moon, and especially the Navamsa (D9). The application flags patterns rather than declaring an inevitable outcome.")
    elif domain == "career":
        notes.append("The 10th/6th/11th combination is used for work, visible contribution and gains; D10 adds a secondary career lens.")
    elif domain == "wellness":
        notes.append("The 1st, 6th, 8th and 12th houses connect vitality, routines, recovery and transformation themes in the traditional reading.")
    elif domain == "spiritual":
        notes.append("The 8th/9th/12th houses are treated as a symbolic triad for transformation, dharma/learning and retreat/inner life.")
    return {"domain": domain, "houses": relevant, "notes": notes}

_NAKSHATRA_INTERP = {
    "Ashwini":"swift beginnings, healing, initiative and a preference for direct movement",
    "Bharani":"capacity, responsibility, endurance, boundaries and transformation through experience",
    "Krittika":"discernment, purification, decisiveness and the ability to separate what is useful from what is not",
    "Rohini":"growth, cultivation, beauty, attraction and the building of tangible value",
    "Mrigashira":"searching, curiosity, investigation and movement toward something not yet fully understood",
    "Ardra":"intensity, renewal, questioning, disruption and rebuilding after insight",
