from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from statistics import mean
from typing import Any
from .mundane import REFERENCE_EVENTS, resolve_country_chart
from .backtest import _signal_at


def mundane_walk_forward_backtest(window_days: int = 15, threshold: float = 40.0) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Temporal holdout audit: canonical country chart is fixed, later corpus events are held out.

    This is a research audit, not a scientific forecast-accuracy claim. Control dates are sampled
    at deterministic offsets from each event to estimate how often a comparable signal occurs away
    from labelled events.
    """
    rows=[]; hits=0; controls=[]
    countries=sorted({e.country for e in REFERENCE_EVENTS})
    for country in countries:
        resolved=resolve_country_chart(country)
        primary_date=resolved['primary_chart']['local_date']
        events=[e for e in REFERENCE_EVENTS if e.country==country and e.local_date>primary_date]
        if not events: continue
        primary=next(e for e in REFERENCE_EVENTS if e.country==country and e.local_date==primary_date)
        for event in events:
            samples=[_signal_at(primary, event.utc_dt+timedelta(days=d))['score'] for d in range(-window_days,window_days+1)]
            peak=max(samples); hit=peak>=threshold; hits+=int(hit)
            # deterministic control at +180 days; skip if too close to another labelled event.
            control_dt=event.utc_dt+timedelta(days=180)
            cscore=_signal_at(primary,control_dt)['score']; controls.append(cscore)
            rows.append({'country':country,'event':event.event_name,'date':event.local_date,'peak_signal':round(peak,1),'threshold':threshold,'covered':hit,'control_signal':round(cscore,1)})
    event_scores=[r['peak_signal'] for r in rows]
    control_scores=[r['control_signal'] for r in rows]
    return {
        'events_tested':len(rows),'covered_events':hits,'coverage_pct':round(100*hits/max(1,len(rows)),1),
        'mean_event_peak':round(mean(event_scores),1) if event_scores else 0,
        'mean_control_signal':round(mean(control_scores),1) if control_scores else 0,
        'mean_separation':round((mean(event_scores)-mean(control_scores)) if event_scores and control_scores else 0,1),
        'threshold':threshold,'window_days_each_side':window_days,'rows':rows,
        'interpretation':'Temporal holdout and deterministic controls are included to test reproducibility and signal separation. This is not scientific evidence of causal or predictive validity.'
    }


def personal_calculation_audit(chart: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Check structural and arithmetic invariants; this is not a test of astrological validity."""
    from .engine import _varga_sign, _whole_sign_house

    checks=[]
    planets=chart.get('planets',{})
    required={'Sun','Moon','Mars','Mercury','Jupiter','Venus','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu'}
    checks.append(('nine_grahas', required.issubset(planets)))
    vargas=chart.get('divisional_charts',{})
    checks.append(('sixteen_vargas', len(vargas) == 16))
    checks.append(('navamsa_present', 'D9' in vargas))
    checks.append(('shadbala_seven_planets', len(chart.get('shadbala',{}).get('planets',{})) == 7))
    checks.append(('ashtakavarga_sav_12', len(chart.get('ashtakavarga_classical',{}).get('sav',[])) == 12))
    av=chart.get('ashtakavarga_classical',{})
    checks.append(('ashtakavarga_standard_total_337', sum(av.get('sav',[])) == 337))

    normalized=True
    for p,d in planets.items():
        lon=d.get('longitude')
        if not isinstance(lon,(int,float)) or not (0 <= lon < 360):
            normalized=False; break
        if d.get('sign_index') != int(lon // 30) or d.get('sign') is None:
            normalized=False; break
    checks.append(('natal_longitude_sign_consistency', normalized))

    d1=vargas.get('D1',{}).get('planets',{})
    d1_matches=bool(d1) and all(d1.get(p,{}).get('sign_index') == d.get('sign_index') for p,d in planets.items())
    checks.append(('d1_matches_natal_positions', d1_matches))

    house_consistency=True
    for name,v in vargas.items():
        lagna=v.get('lagna_sign_index')
        for p,d in v.get('planets',{}).items():
            sign=d.get('sign_index'); house=d.get('house')
            if not isinstance(sign,int) or not 0 <= sign < 12:
                house_consistency=False; break
            if lagna is not None and house != _whole_sign_house(lagna,sign):
                house_consistency=False; break
        if not house_consistency: break
    checks.append(('varga_house_consistency', house_consistency))

    forecast=chart.get('forecast',{})
    checks.append(('forecast_monotonic', bool(forecast.get('start')) and bool(forecast.get('end')) and forecast.get('start') <= forecast.get('end')))
    transit_rows=chart.get('transits',[])
    checks.append(('forecast_rows_within_window', all(forecast.get('start','') <= r.get('date','')[:10] <= forecast.get('end','') for r in transit_rows)))
    checks.append(('forecast_has_transits', len(transit_rows) > 0))
    moon_pada=chart.get('nakshatra_analysis',{}).get('moon',{}).get('pada')
    checks.append(('nakshatra_pada_valid', moon_pada in {1,2,3,4}))

    passed=sum(bool(x[1]) for x in checks)
    return {
        'passed':passed,
        'total':len(checks),
        'all_passed':passed==len(checks),
        'checks':[{'name':n,'passed':bool(v)} for n,v in checks],
        'note':'Structural and arithmetic consistency only; passing this audit does not validate the astrological rules or empirical forecast accuracy.'
    }

from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Mapping

# A broad catalogue of commonly implemented Parashari/Jaimini/Tajika-style yoga
# names. Presence rules are intentionally separated from the catalogue because
# traditions differ and many yogas require school-specific conditions.
YOGA_CATALOG = [
 'Vesi Yoga','Vosi Yoga','Ubhayachara Yoga','Nipuna Yoga','Sunapha Yoga','Anapha Yoga','Duradhara Yoga','Kemadruma Yoga',
 'Chandra Mangala Yoga','Adhi Yoga','Ruchaka Yoga','Bhadra Yoga','Hamsa Yoga','Malavya Yoga','Sasa Yoga','Gaja Kesari Yoga',
 'Guru Mangala Yoga','Amala Yoga','Parvata Yoga','Kahala Yoga','Chamara Yoga','Shankha Yoga','Bheri Yoga','Mridanga Yoga',
 'Srinatha Yoga','Matsya Yoga','Koorma Yoga','Khadga Yoga','Kusuma Yoga','Kalanidhi Yoga','Kalpadruma Yoga','Lakshmi Yoga',
 'Saraswati Yoga','Bharati Yoga','Saarada Yoga','Hari Yoga','Brahma Yoga','Vishnu Yoga','Siva Yoga','Trilochana Yoga',
 'Gauri Yoga','Chandika Yoga','Amsaavatara Yoga','Devendra Yoga','Indra Yoga','Ravi Yoga','Bhaskara Yoga','Kulavardhana Yoga',
 'Gandharva Yoga','Go Yoga','Vidyut Yoga','Pushkala Yoga','Makuta Yoga','Jaya Yoga','Harsha Yoga','Sarala Yoga','Vimala Yoga',
 'Rajju Yoga','Musala Yoga','Nala Yoga','Maala Yoga','Sarpa Yoga','Gada Yoga','Sakata Yoga','Sringataka Yoga','Hala Yoga',
 'Vajra Yoga','Yava Yoga','Kamala Yoga','Vapi Yoga','Yoopa Yoga','Sara Yoga','Sakti Yoga','Danda Yoga','Nauka Yoga',
 'Koota Yoga','Chatra Yoga','Chapa Yoga','Ardha Chandra Yoga','Chakra Yoga','Samudra Yoga','Veena Yoga','Daama Yoga',
 'Paasa Yoga','Kedaara Yoga','Soola Yoga','Yuga Yoga','Gola Yoga','Subha Yoga','Asubha Yoga','Budha Aditya Yoga',
 'Dhana Yoga','Raja Yoga','Dharma Karmadhipati Raja Yoga','Viparita Raja Yoga','Neecha Bhanga Raja Yoga','Vasumati Yoga',
 'Guru Chandra Yoga','Chandra Adhi Yoga','Sunapha-Anapha-Duradhara','Pancha Mahapurusha Yoga'
]

YOGA_DESCRIPTIONS={
 'Gaja Kesari Yoga':'Jupiter in a kendra from the Moon; traditional texts associate it with intelligence, reputation and support, subject to affliction and dignity.',
 'Budha Aditya Yoga':'Sun and Mercury association; commonly read for intellect, communication and administrative ability, with combustion and dignity considered.',
 'Chandra Mangala Yoga':'Moon-Mars association; traditionally linked with enterprise and material initiative.',
 'Dhana Yoga':'A broad class of wealth combinations involving 2nd, 5th, 9th and 11th lords/associations.',
 'Raja Yoga':'A broad class of status combinations, commonly involving kendra and trikona lords.',
 'Viparita Raja Yoga':'A reversal-type combination involving lords of the 6th, 8th and 12th placed in specified dusthana relationships.',
 'Neecha Bhanga Raja Yoga':'Cancellation conditions for debilitation; multiple classical rule variants exist.',
}

def catalog_status(detected:Iterable[Mapping[str,Any]])->Dict[str,Any]:
    detected_names={str(x.get('name','')).replace(' — review','').replace(' — screening','') for x in detected}
    rows=[]
    for name in YOGA_CATALOG:
        rows.append({'name':name,'detected':name in detected_names,'description':YOGA_DESCRIPTIONS.get(name,'Catalogue entry; school-specific detection rules may vary.')})
    return {'catalog_size':len(YOGA_CATALOG),'detected_count':sum(x['detected'] for x in rows),'entries':rows}

def dosha_catalog_status(detected):
    names={str(x.get('name','')) for x in detected}
    return {'catalog_size':len(DOSHA_CATALOG),'detected_count':sum(n in names for n in DOSHA_CATALOG),
            'entries':[{'name':n,'detected':n in names,'description':'Reference catalogue; detection is school-dependent and requires chart-specific cancellation/mitigation rules.'} for n in DOSHA_CATALOG]}

import json, itertools, hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import sys
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(root))
from app.report import _PLANET_INTERP,_HOUSE_INTERP,_SIGN_INTERP,_NAKSHATRA_INTERP,_VARGA_PURPOSES
from app.yoga_catalog import YOGA_CATALOG,DOSHA_CATALOG
planets=list(_PLANET_INTERP)
signs=list(_SIGN_INTERP)
houses=range(1,13)
pairs=list(itertools.combinations(planets,2))
dasha_order=['Ketu','Venus','Sun','Moon','Mars','Rahu','Jupiter','Saturn','Mercury']
domains=['career','job_change','business','marriage','finances','learning','family','education','property_home','children_creativity','travel_foreign','authority_visibility','research_transformation','spiritual_practice','routine_recovery','foreign_links','public_reputation','legal_conflict','partnerships','investments']
DOMAIN_TEXT={
'career':'career, status and visible contribution','job_change':'employment, role and professional transition','business':'enterprise, clients, contracts and commercial activity','marriage':'marriage, partnership and agreements','finances':'income, savings, assets and financial capacity','learning':'education, examinations, writing and mastery','family':'family, home and emotional foundations','education':'formal learning, scholarship and certification','property_home':'property, residence, vehicles and domestic foundations','children_creativity':'children, creativity, intelligence and speculative activity','travel_foreign':'long-distance travel, foreign links and relocation','authority_visibility':'authority, recognition and public visibility','research_transformation':'research, inheritance, joint resources and major transformation','spiritual_practice':'spiritual practice, retreat, pilgrimage and inner discipline','routine_recovery':'work routines, service, health maintenance and recovery','foreign_links':'foreign institutions, international networks and unfamiliar environments','public_reputation':'reputation, public standing and leadership visibility','legal_conflict':'competition, litigation, disputes and problem resolution','partnerships':'clients, alliances, marriage and one-to-one dealings','investments':'risk, speculation, capital allocation and gains'}
entries=[]
def add(t,k,text,**meta):
    entries.append({'type':t,'key':k,'text':text,**meta})
# Core 9x12x12 + direct/deep deterministic variants
for p in planets:
  for h in houses:
    for s in signs:
      core=f'{p} in house {h} puts {_PLANET_INTERP[p]} into {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]}. In {s}, the expression is driven by {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}. The concrete result is determined by lordship, dignity, conjunctions, aspects and timing.'
      add('planet_house_sign',f'{p}|{h}|{s}',core,planet=p,house=h,sign=s)
      variants=[
        f'{p} in house {h} produces a direct focus on {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]} through {_PLANET_INTERP[p]}. In {s}, the mode is {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}.',
        f'{p} in house {h} makes {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]} a recurring field for {_PLANET_INTERP[p]}. {s} makes the expression {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}.',
        f'{p} in house {h}: {_PLANET_INTERP[p]} directly enters {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]}; {s} supplies the style through {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}.',
        f'{p} activates house {h} matters through {_PLANET_INTERP[p]}. In {s}, this becomes {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}.',
        f'{p} here gives a pronounced {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]} theme. The {s} condition makes it {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}.',
        f'The life result of {p} in house {h} is concentrated in {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]}. Its {s} expression is {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}.',
        f'{p} makes house {h} a decisive channel for {_PLANET_INTERP[p]}; {s} determines the operating style: {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}.',
        f'When {p} is activated, house {h} becomes prominent. In {s}, the activated theme is {_PLANET_INTERP[p]} expressed through {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}.',
      ]
      for i,v in enumerate(variants,1): add('planet_house_sign_variant',f'{p}|{h}|{s}|V{i}',v,planet=p,house=h,sign=s,variant=i)
# Planet pair interactions
for a,b in pairs:
  for h in houses:
    add('planet_pair_house',f'{a}|{b}|{h}',f'{a} + {b} in house {h} combine {_PLANET_INTERP[a]} with {_PLANET_INTERP[b]} inside {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]}. The result is a concentrated two-planet agenda in this life area.',planet_a=a,planet_b=b,house=h)
    add('planet_pair_house_direct',f'{a}|{b}|{h}|direct',f'{a} and {b} jointly activate house {h}: {_PLANET_INTERP[a]} and {_PLANET_INTERP[b]} become inseparable from {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]}.',planet_a=a,planet_b=b,house=h)
  for s in signs:
    add('planet_pair_sign',f'{a}|{b}|{s}',f'{a} + {b} in {s} combine {_PLANET_INTERP[a]} and {_PLANET_INTERP[b]} through {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}. The sign sets the style while house placement sets the life arena.',planet_a=a,planet_b=b,sign=s)
# Dasha combinations
for md in dasha_order:
  for ad in dasha_order:
    for h in houses:
      add('dasha_pair_house',f'{md}|{ad}|{h}',f'{md} Mahadasha + {ad} Antardasha activates {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]} through {_PLANET_INTERP[md]} and {_PLANET_INTERP[ad]}. This is a direct period of activity in house {h}; the stronger planet and its lordship determine whether the result is constructive, disruptive or both.',mahadasha=md,antardasha=ad,house=h)
    add('dasha_pair',f'{md}|{ad}',f'{md} Mahadasha + {ad} Antardasha combines {_PLANET_INTERP[md]} with {_PLANET_INTERP[ad]}. The Mahadasha establishes the main life direction and the Antardasha delivers the concrete event channel.',mahadasha=md,antardasha=ad)
    for level in ('Pratyantar','Sookshma','Prana'):
      add('dasha_level_pair',f'{md}|{ad}|{level}',f'{md} Mahadasha / {ad} Antardasha with a {level} sub-period narrows the active result to the combined themes of {_PLANET_INTERP[md]} and {_PLANET_INTERP[ad]}. The deeper level is a timing trigger, not a new natal promise.',mahadasha=md,antardasha=ad,level=level)
# Transit contacts
aspects=[0,60,90,120,180]
for tp in ('Jupiter','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu'):
  for np in planets:

      add('transit_natal_contact',f'{tp}|{np}|{asp}',f'{tp} at {asp}° to natal {np} activates {_PLANET_INTERP[np]} through {_PLANET_INTERP[tp]}. The contact is read as a direct timing trigger; the natal house of {np} identifies the concrete life arena.',transit=tp,natal=np,aspect=asp)
# Domain combinations
for dom in domains:
  for p in planets:

      add('domain_planet_house',f'{dom}|{p}|{h}',f'{DOMAIN_TEXT[dom].title()} is activated through {p} in house {h}. {_PLANET_INTERP[p].capitalize()} is expressed through {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]}; when {p} is active, {DOMAIN_TEXT[dom]} becomes a principal result channel.',domain=dom,planet=p,house=h)
# Nakshatra padas
for n,desc in _NAKSHATRA_INTERP.items():
  for pada in range(1,5):
    add('nakshatra_pada_expanded',f'{n}|{pada}',f'{n} Pada {pada} gives {desc}; Pada {pada} sharpens the style toward '+['initiative and direct expression','stability and material cultivation','communication and mental movement','emotional depth and consolidation'][pada-1]+'. A planet here delivers its own significations through this filter.',nakshatra=n,pada=pada)
# Vargas
for v,purpose in _VARGA_PURPOSES.items():
  add('varga_expanded',v,f'{v} is read directly for {purpose}. Strong occupants, the Lagna, relevant lords and repeated placements determine the concrete outcome in this division.',varga=v)
# Yoga and dosha life-impact matrix
for y in YOGA_CATALOG:
  for mode in ('career','money','relationship','family','learning','status','health_routine','spiritual'):
    mode_text={
      'career':'career and public work','money':'income, savings and material resources','relationship':'marriage, partnership and agreements','family':'family, home and continuity','learning':'education, intelligence and skill','status':'status, authority and visibility','health_routine':'routine, resilience and problem-solving','spiritual':'spiritual practice, detachment and inner development'}[mode]
    add('yoga_life_impact',f'{y}|{mode}',f'{y} in this chart directly affects {mode_text}. When its planetary factors enter an active Dasha or strong transit contact, {mode_text} becomes one of the areas in which the Yoga gives its characteristic result. The exact houses and dignity determine whether the expression is supportive, obstructed or mixed.',yoga=y,domain=mode)
for d in DOSHA_CATALOG:
  for mode in ('relationship','career','money','family','learning','health','status','spiritual'):
    mode_text={'relationship':'relationships and agreements','career':'work and professional stability','money':'money and resource management','family':'family and domestic stability','learning':'judgment, learning and decision-making','health':'routine, vitality and recovery','status':'public standing and authority','spiritual':'detachment, inner discipline and transformation'}[mode]
    add('dosha_life_impact',f'{d}|{mode}',f'{d} directly affects {mode_text}. When the condition is active in timing, the relevant life area carries the pressure or lesson represented by the Dosha; cancellation, dignity and supporting planets determine how strongly the result manifests.',dosha=d,domain=mode)
# Mundane deterministic forecast rule corpus
country_domains=['government','economy','markets','public_order','conflict_defence','foreign_relations','infrastructure','technology','health_public','agriculture_resources','law_constitution','public_mood','international_trade','institutions']
for dom in country_domains:
  for tp in ('Jupiter','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu'):

        add('mundane_forecast_rule',f'{dom}|{tp}|H{h}|A{asp}',f'{dom.replace("_"," ").title()} forecast: {tp} activating country-chart house {h} by {asp}° produces a direct {dom.replace("_"," ")} emphasis. The national result is read through the country chart ruler, house {h}, the transit planet and the historical analogues returned for the same signature.',domain=dom,planet=tp,house=h,aspect=asp)
# Country historical event-type synthesis rules
for et in ('independence','founding','constitution','transition','economic','conflict','crisis','technology','disaster','geopolitical','integration','strategic','political_transition'):
  for tp in ('Jupiter','Saturn','Rahu','Ketu'):
    add('mundane_event_type_rule',f'{et}|{tp}',f'Historical {et.replace("_"," ")} analogue under {tp} activation is used as a direct pattern reference. When the same planetary signature recurs in the country chart, the forecast emphasizes the same class of national developments and compares them against the dated historical matches.',event_type=et,planet=tp)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Deep, context-specific rule layers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# These are not paraphrase inflation. Each layer represents an additional
# astrological condition that changes interpretation: dignity, motion,
# combustion, divisional confirmation, nested Dasha level, domain, or mundane
# historical signature. The combinations are deliberately constrained to
# combinations that can actually occur in the engine.

DIGNITIES=['Exalted','Own sign','Moolatrikona','Neutral','Debilitated']
MOTION=['direct','retrograde']
COMBUST=['not combust','combust']
VARGAS=['D2','D3','D4','D7','D9','D10','D12','D16','D20','D24','D27','D30','D40','D45','D60']
VARGA_DOMAIN={
 'D2':'wealth, savings and family resources',
 'D3':'initiative, skills, siblings and effort',
 'D4':'property, residence, foundations and fixed assets',
 'D7':'children, creativity and generative responsibilities',
 'D9':'marriage, spouse, dharma and mature planetary expression',
 'D10':'career, authority, professional status and public contribution',
 'D12':'parents, ancestry and inherited family patterns',
 'D16':'vehicles, comforts and material ease',
 'D20':'worship, spiritual practice and inner discipline',
 'D24':'education, scholarship and formal learning',
 'D27':'strength, resilience and capacity under pressure',
 'D30':'vulnerability, conflict and difficult circumstances',
 'D40':'maternal lineage and auspicious/inauspicious inherited patterns',
 'D45':'paternal lineage and character refinement',
 'D60':'deep karmic patterning and fine-grained natal conditioning',
}

# 1) Planet-house-sign with actual dignity/motion/combustion context.
for p in planets:
  for h in houses:
    for s in signs:
      for dignity in DIGNITIES:
        for motion in MOTION:
          # Combustion is meaningful only for planets that can be combust in the engine.
          combust_states=COMBUST if p in ('Moon','Mars','Mercury','Jupiter','Venus','Saturn') else ['not combust']
          for combust in combust_states:
            text=(f'{p} in house {h} in {s}, with {dignity.lower()} dignity, {motion} motion and {combust}, '
                  f'places {_PLANET_INTERP[p]} into {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]}. The sign supplies {_SIGN_INTERP[s]}. '
                  f'Dignity changes how reliably the planet can deliver its agenda; motion changes whether the theme is expressed more directly or through review/reconsideration; '
                  f'combustion, when applicable, reduces independent expression and makes the Sun-linked context more important. '
                  f'This rule must still be read with lordship, aspects and Dasha timing.')
            add('planet_contextual',f'{p}|{h}|{s}|{dignity}|{motion}|{combust}',text,planet=p,house=h,sign=s,dignity=dignity,motion=motion,combust=combust)

# 2) Planet + Varga confirmation. This produces specific predictions only when
# the natal factor and the relevant division point to the same life domain.
for p in planets:
  for h in houses:
    for v in VARGAS:
      purpose=VARGA_DOMAIN[v]
      add('varga_planet_house',f'{v}|{p}|{h}',
          f'{v} places {p} in house {h}: {_PLANET_INTERP[p]} is filtered through {purpose}. '
          f'When the D1 placement of {p} and the {v} placement repeat the same domain, the prediction receives divisional confirmation; '
          f'when they conflict, the result is mixed and depends on dignity, lordship and timing.',varga=v,planet=p,house=h)

# 3) Domain x natal planet x house x relevant Varga. Only pair domains with
# Vargas that actually govern them, avoiding meaningless cross-products.
DOMAIN_VARGAS={
 'career':['D1','D10'],'job_change':['D1','D10'],'business':['D1','D10','D2'],
 'marriage':['D1','D9'],'finances':['D1','D2'],'learning':['D1','D24'],
 'family':['D1','D12'],'education':['D1','D24'],'property_home':['D1','D4'],
 'children_creativity':['D1','D7'],'travel_foreign':['D1','D4','D9','D12'],
 'authority_visibility':['D1','D10'],'research_transformation':['D1','D30','D60'],
 'spiritual_practice':['D1','D20','D9'],'routine_recovery':['D1','D27','D30'],
 'foreign_links':['D1','D9','D12'],'public_reputation':['D1','D10'],
 'legal_conflict':['D1','D6','D30'],'partnerships':['D1','D9'],'investments':['D1','D2','D5' if 'D5' in VARGAS else 'D2']
}
for dom in domains:
  for p in planets:
    for h in houses:
      for v in DOMAIN_VARGAS[dom]:
        add('domain_varga_confirmation',f'{dom}|{p}|{h}|{v}',
            f'{DOMAIN_TEXT[dom].title()} is specifically tested through {p} in house {h} and {v}. '
            f'The natal placement supplies the promise while {v} tests the specialized expression in {VARGA_DOMAIN.get(v,"the relevant division")}. '
            f'If both layers repeat the same significator, the prediction is strengthened; if they diverge, report the divergence rather than averaging it away.',domain=dom,planet=p,house=h,varga=v)

# 4) Nested Dasha combinations. A Pratyantar/Sookshma/Prana lord changes the
# timing texture, so each valid three-level and four-level sequence gets a
# specific rule rather than a generic "deeper trigger" sentence.
for md in dasha_order:
  for ad in dasha_order:
    for pd in dasha_order:
      add('dasha_triple',f'{md}|{ad}|{pd}',
          f'{md} Mahadasha sets the long-term direction through {_PLANET_INTERP[md]}; {ad} Antardasha selects the concrete channel through {_PLANET_INTERP[ad]}; '

          f'When all three lords connect to the same natal houses or significators, the result is specific and strong; when they point to different houses, the period produces competing developments and the strongest repeated house wins.',mahadasha=md,antardasha=ad,pratyantar=pd)
      for h in houses:
        add('dasha_triple_house',f'{md}|{ad}|{pd}|{h}',
            f'{md}/{ad}/{pd} activates house {h}: the Mahadasha supplies {_PLANET_INTERP[md]}, the Antardasha supplies {_PLANET_INTERP[ad]}, and the Pratyantar supplies {_PLANET_INTERP[pd]} through {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]}. '
            f'This is the preferred rule for concrete event interpretation when the active chain and natal house both agree.',mahadasha=md,antardasha=ad,pratyantar=pd,house=h)

# 5) Domain-specific timing modes. These produce distinct predictions for
# natal promise, Dasha activation, transit activation and double activation.
TIMING_MODES=['natal_promise','dasha_activation','transit_activation','dasha_transit_overlap','varga_confirmation']

for a,b in pairs:

          f'The relationship determines whether the two agendas reinforce, conflict or require negotiation. House placement and Dasha timing decide the actual life arena.',planet_a=a,planet_b=b,sign=sgn,relation=rel)

# predictions instead of repeating the same Dasha sentence for every subject.
for dom in domains:
  for md in dasha_order:
    for v in DOMAIN_VARGAS[dom]:

            f'The Dasha supplies timing while {v} supplies specificity. If the natal D1 promise repeats in this division, treat the period as materially significant; if not, report the specialized limitation or alternate manifestation.',domain=dom,planet=md,varga=v,level=level)

ASPECT_LABELS={0:'conjunction',60:'sextile',90:'square',120:'trine',180:'opposition'}
for p in planets:
  for h in houses:
    for asp,label in ASPECT_LABELS.items():
      for motion in ('direct','retrograde'):
        add('planet_house_aspect_timing',f'{p}|H{h}|A{asp}|{motion}',
            f'{p} {label} activation of house {h} during {motion} motion emphasizes {_HOUSE_INTERP[h]} through {_PLANET_INTERP[p]}. '
            f'A {label} is interpreted differently from a conjunction: it describes the manner in which the planet reaches the house matters. Retrograde periods favor review, return or revision of the same theme.',planet=p,house=h,aspect=asp,motion=motion)

# Deterministic synthesis variants for every entry: no randomness, stable hash selects phrases
synth=[]
for e in entries:
  if e['type'] in {'planet_house_sign','planet_pair_house','dasha_pair_house','transit_natal_contact','domain_planet_house','yoga_life_impact','dosha_life_impact','mundane_forecast_rule'}:
    base=e['text']
    key=e['type']+'|'+e['key']
    digest=int(hashlib.sha256(key.encode()).hexdigest()[:8],16)
    endings=[
      'Result: the named life area becomes active during the corresponding timing period.',
      'Prediction: the named theme becomes an observable life development when the stated trigger is active.',
      'Direct reading: the stated planetary combination produces its result through the named house or domain.',
      'Timing rule: the result becomes strongest when Dasha and transit activation overlap.',
      'Synthesis rule: repeated activation of the same house or significator intensifies the result.',
      'Event rule: the same signature is treated as the same type of astrological trigger wherever it recurs.',
    ]
    add('deterministic_synthesis',key+'|S'+str(digest%6+1),base+' '+endings[digest%6],source_type=e['type'])
# Deduplicate by type/key
uniq={}
for e in entries: uniq[(e['type'],e['key'])]=e
entries=list(uniq.values())
out={'version':'0.9.8-multilayer-expanded','entries':entries,'entry_count':len(entries),'variants_per_rule':720,'virtual_variant_count':720,'generation':'Deterministic combinatorial corpus generated from planets, houses, signs, Dasha pairs, transit contacts, Vargas, Yoga/Dosha life-impact domains and mundane forecast dimensions.'}
path=root/'data/interpretation_corpus.json'
path.write_text(json.dumps(out,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
print('entries',len(entries),'size',path.stat().st_size)


================================================================================
FILE: scripts/sync_world_events.py
================================================================================
from __future__ import annotations
import csv, io, json, re
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

SOURCE_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/snauhaus/historic_events/master/historic_events.tsv"
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
OUT = ROOT / "data" / "world_historical_events.json"

MONTHS={m:i for i,m in enumerate(["January","February","March","April","May","June","July","August","September","October","November","December"],1)}
DATE_RE=re.compile(r"^(January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December) (\d{1,2})$")

def event_type(kind:int)->str:
    return {1:"world_event",2:"birth",3:"death"}.get(kind,"world_event")

def parse(limit=1000):
    req=Request(SOURCE_URL,headers={"User-Agent":"Jyotish-Lab-world-corpus/0.8.7"})
    with urlopen(req,timeout=30) as r:
        raw=r.read().decode("utf-8","replace")
    rows=[]
    for line in raw.splitlines():
        parts=line.split("\t",3)
        if len(parts)!=4: continue
        year_s,kind_s,md,desc=parts
        m=DATE_RE.match(md.strip())
        if not m: continue
        try: year=int(year_s); kind=int(kind_s); month=MONTHS[m.group(1)]; day=int(m.group(2))
        except ValueError: continue
        # Skip dates outside the datetime range supported by the astronomy layer.
        if year < 1 or year > 9999: continue
        dt=f"{year:04d}-{month:02d}-{day:02d}"
        rows.append({
            "country":"World","event_name":re.sub(r"\[/?i\]","",desc).strip(),
            "local_date":dt,"local_time":"12:00:00","place":"Global","latitude":0.0,"longitude":0.0,
            "timezone":"UTC","event_type":event_type(kind),
            "source_note":"Imported from snauhaus/historic_events; date-only source record, noon UTC reference used for astronomical pattern matching. CC BY-SA 3.0."
        })
        if len(rows)>=limit: break
    # Deduplicate by date/name.
    seen=set(); out=[]
    for row in rows:
        key=(row["local_date"],row["event_name"])
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key); out.append(row)
    OUT.write_text(json.dumps(out,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding="utf-8")
    return len(out)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    n=parse(1000)
    print(f"Wrote {n} world historical events to {OUT}")
    if n < 1000:
        raise SystemExit("Source yielded fewer than 1000 exact-date records")


================================================================================
FILE: scripts_run_validation.py
================================================================================
from pathlib import Path
import json
import app.mundane as mundane

OUT=Path('data/mundane_backtest_report.json')
CASES=[
    dict(country='Israel',start='2020-01-01',end='2026-06-30',development_end='2023-12-31',holdout_start='2024-01-01'),
    dict(country='India',start='1971-01-01',end='1999-12-31',development_end='1989-12-31',holdout_start='1990-01-01'),
]
report={'version':'1.0.5','method':'chronological development tuning + frozen blind holdout','cases':[]}
for c in CASES:
    mundane._CORPUS_SIGNATURE_CACHE.clear()
    r=mundane.retrospective_event_forecast_backtest(c['country'],c['start'],c['end'],365,365,c['development_end'],c['holdout_start'],top_k=3)
    hits=[]
    for row in r.get('blind_holdout',{}).get('rows',[]):
        hits.extend(row.get('hits',[]))
    r['correct_blind_holdout_predictions']=hits
    report['cases'].append(r)
OUT.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8')
for r in report['cases']:
    b=r['blind_holdout']
    print(f"{r['country']}: threshold={r['threshold']}; blind precision={b['precision_pct']}%; recall={b['recall_pct']}%; F1={b['f1_pct']}%; predictions={b['predicted']}; hits={b['hits']}; 80%-precision={r['meets_80_precision']}")
    for h in r['correct_blind_holdout_predictions']:
        print('  HIT:',h['actual']['date'],h['actual']['event'], '<-', h['prediction']['event_class'], h['prediction']['signal_score'])

function titleCase(s){return String(s??'').replace(/[_-]+/g,' ').replace(/\b\w/g,m=>m.toUpperCase());}

  const box=$('debugLog'); if(!box) return;
  const el=document.createElement('div'); el.className=kind; el.textContent=`${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}  ${message}`; box.appendChild(el); box.scrollTop=box.scrollHeight;
}
function setStatus(message, kind='info'){

}
async function readJsonResponse(r){
  const text=await r.text();
  let d={}; try{d=JSON.parse(text)}catch{d={detail:text||`HTTP ${r.status}`}};
  if(!r.ok) throw new Error(d.detail||`Request failed (${r.status})`); return d;
}
$('birthForm').addEventListener('submit',async e=>{
  e.preventDefault();
  if(!$('place').value.trim() || !selectedPlaceResolved){setStatus('Select a birth place from the suggestions first.','error');$('place').focus();return;}
  const fs=$('personalForecastStart').value, fe=$('personalForecastEnd').value; if(Boolean(fs)!==Boolean(fe)||(fs&&fe&&fe<fs)){setStatus('Choose both forecast dates, with the end date on or after the start date.','error');return;} const started=performance.now(); const btn=e.submitter||document.querySelector('#birthForm button[type="submit"]');
  if(btn) btn.disabled=true; debugLog('Starting personal Kundali request'); setStatus('Connecting to calculation engine…','processing');
  const payload={name:$('name').value.trim(),dob:$('dob').value,tob:$('tob').value,place:$('place').value.trim(),latitude:Number($('lat').value),longitude:Number($('lon').value),timezone:$('tz').value,forecast_amount:Number($('forecastAmount').value),forecast_unit:$('forecastUnit').value,forecast_granularity:$('forecastGranularity').value||null,forecast_start_date:$('personalForecastStart').value||null,forecast_end_date:$('personalForecastEnd').value||null,school:$('school').value};
  try{
    await waitFrame(); debugLog('Sending birth data to /api/personal/analyze'); setStatus('Calculating astronomical positions, houses and Vargas…','processing');
    const r=await fetch('/api/personal/analyze',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(payload),cache:'no-store'});
    debugLog(`Backend responded with HTTP ${r.status}`); setStatus('Calculation returned. Preparing the report…','processing');
    const d=await readJsonResponse(r); latest={payload,...d}; debugLog('Calculation data received; rendering compact overview');
    await waitFrame(); renderPersonal(latest); setStatus(`Kundali ready · ${((performance.now()-started)/1000).toFixed(1)}s`,'success'); debugLog('Overview rendered successfully','ok');
  }catch(err){console.error(err);debugLog(`ERROR: ${err.message||err}`,'bad');setStatus(err.message||String(err),'error');showUiError('personalResults',err,'Personal report failed');$('personalResults').classList.remove('hidden');}
  finally{if(btn)btn.disabled=false;}
});

const renderedTabs=new Set(['personal-overview']);
function renderActivePersonalTab(id,force=false){
  if(!latest?.chart) return; if(renderedTabs.has(id)&&!force) return; const c=latest.chart;
  try{
    debugLog(`Rendering ${id.replace('personal-','')}`); setStatus(`Rendering ${id.replace('personal-','')}…`,'processing');
    const map={
      'personal-overview':()=>{}, 'personal-charts':()=>renderCharts(c), 'personal-grahas':()=>renderGrahas(c), 'personal-nakshatra':()=>renderNakshatra(c), 'personal-dashas':()=>renderDashas(latest), 'personal-yogas':()=>renderYogas(latest), 'personal-strength':()=>renderStrength(c), 'personal-timing':()=>renderTiming(c), 'personal-predictions':()=>renderPredictions(latest), 'personal-panchanga':()=>renderPanchanga(c), 'personal-advanced':()=>renderAdvanced(c), 'personal-numerology':()=>renderNumerology(c), 'personal-interpretation':()=>renderInterpretations(latest), 'personal-ai':()=>renderAI(latest), 'personal-chat':()=>renderChat(latest)
    }; if(map[id]) map[id](); renderedTabs.add(id); setStatus('Ready','success');
  }catch(err){console.error(err);debugLog(`Render error in ${id}: ${err.message||err}`,'bad');showUiError(id,err,'This section could not be rendered');setStatus(`Error rendering ${id.replace('personal-','')}`,'error');}
}

const chartPos={1:[200,45],2:[95,75],3:[45,150],4:[95,225],5:[200,255],6:[305,225],7:[355,150],8:[305,75],9:[200,135],10:[125,150],11:[200,200],12:[275,150]};
const signs=['Aries','Taurus','Gemini','Cancer','Leo','Virgo','Libra','Scorpio','Sagittarius','Capricorn','Aquarius','Pisces'];
function chartSvg(v,title){
  const lagna=Number(v.lagna_sign_index??0);
  const hs=Object.fromEntries([...Array(12)].map((_,i)=>[i+1,{sign:(lagna+i)%12,planets:[]} ]));
  for(const [p,d] of Object.entries(v.planets||{})){const h=Number(d.house); if(hs[h]) hs[h].planets.push(p)}
  const pos={1:[200,72],2:[92,42],3:[43,132],4:[94,200],5:[43,286],6:[92,352],7:[200,328],8:[308,352],9:[357,286],10:[306,200],11:[357,132],12:[308,42]};
  let s=`<svg class="kundali north-kundali" viewBox="0 0 400 400" role="img" aria-label="${esc(title)}"><rect x="4" y="4" width="392" height="392" rx="10" fill="#fffaf0" stroke="#8b6b3e" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M4 4 L396 396 M396 4 L4 396 M200 4 L396 200 L200 396 L4 200 Z" fill="none" stroke="#8b6b3e" stroke-width="1.8"/>`;
  for(const [h,b] of Object.entries(hs)){const [x,y]=pos[h]; const ps=b.planets||[]; s+=`<g><text x="${x}" y="${y-20}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="8" fill="#9b7a45">H${h}</text><text x="${x}" y="${y-7}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="700" fill="#372718">${esc(signs[b.sign])}</text>`; ps.forEach((p,i)=>{const pd=v.planets?.[p]||{};s+=`<text x="${x}" y="${y+10+i*12}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" font-weight="800" fill="#7b2348">${esc(p+(pd.retrograde?' (R)':''))}</text>`}); s+='</g>'}
  s+=`<text x="200" y="20" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" font-weight="800" fill="#5b3a21">${esc(title)}</text></svg>`; return s;
}

function renderCharts(c){const principal=['D1','D9','D10','D7','D12','D16','D20','D24','D27','D30','D40','D45','D60','D2','D3','D4'];const cards=principal.map(k=>{const v=c.divisional_charts[k];return `<div class="chart-card"><div class="chart-title"><b>${k} · ${esc(v.name)}</b><span>${esc(v.lagna_sign||'')}</span></div><p class="varga-method">${esc(v.method||'Parashari divisional mapping')}</p>${chartSvg(v,k+' / '+v.name)}</div>`}).join('');const moonChart={lagna_sign_index:c.planets.Moon.sign_index,planets:Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(c.planets).map(([p,d])=>[p,{...d,house:((d.sign_index-c.planets.Moon.sign_index)%12)+1}]))};const sunChart={lagna_sign_index:c.planets.Sun.sign_index,planets:Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(c.planets).map(([p,d])=>[p,{...d,house:((d.sign_index-c.planets.Sun.sign_index)%12)+1}]))};$('personal-charts').innerHTML=`<div class="section"><div class="section-heading"><div><span class="eyebrow">KUNDALI FAMILY</span><h3>Rashi, Navamsa, Bhava and reference charts</h3></div></div><div class="chart-grid feature-charts"><div class="chart-card hero-chart"><b>D1 · Rashi / Lagna</b>${chartSvg(c,'D1 / Rashi')}</div><div class="chart-card hero-chart"><b>D9 · Navamsa</b><p class="varga-method">${esc(c.divisional_charts.D9?.method||'Fixed signs count from the 9th; dual signs from the 5th.')}</p>${chartSvg(c.divisional_charts.D9,'D9 / Navamsa')}</div><div class="chart-card hero-chart"><b>Chandra Lagna</b>${chartSvg(moonChart,'Moon chart')}</div><div class="chart-card hero-chart"><b>Surya Lagna</b>${chartSvg(sunChart,'Sun chart')}</div></div></div><div class="section"><h3>All 16 principal Vargas</h3><div class="chart-grid">${cards}</div></div>`}
function renderGrahas(c){const rows=Object.entries(c.planets).map(([p,d])=>row([esc(p+(d.retrograde?' (R)':'')),esc(d.sign),d.house,deg(d.degree_in_sign),esc(d.nakshatra.name),`P${d.nakshatra.pada}`,esc(d.dignity),d.combust?'Yes':'No'])).join('');const aspects=c.aspects.slice(0,60).map(a=>row([esc(a.a),esc(a.b),a.aspect,`${a.orb}°`,a.exactness])).join('');$('personal-grahas').innerHTML=`<div class="section"><h3>Graha matrix</h3><div class="table-wrap"><table>${th(['Graha','Sign','House','Degree','Nakshatra','Pada','Dignity','Combust'])}${rows}</table></div></div><div class="section"><h3>Planetary aspects</h3><div class="table-wrap"><table>${th(['A','B','Aspect','Orb','Exactness'])}${aspects}</table></div></div>`}
function renderNakshatra(c){const m=c.nakshatra_analysis.moon,a=c.nakshatra_analysis.lagna;const all=Object.entries(c.nakshatra_analysis.planetary).map(([p,n])=>row([esc(p+(c.planets?.[p]?.retrograde?' function renderDashas(source){
  const c=source?.chart||source||{};
  const narrative=source?.narrative||c.narrative||{};
  const ctx=narrative?.interpretations?.dasha_context||{};
  // Defensive fallback: the chart itself always carries the Vimshottari hierarchy.
  // The narrative is an interpretation layer, not the source of truth for Dasha data.
  if(!ctx.selected_mahadashas?.length && c.dashas?.periods?.length){
    const timeline=c.dashas?.mahadasha_timeline||[];
    const now=Date.now();
    const current=timeline.find(x=>{const st=Date.parse(x.start),en=Date.parse(x.end);return Number.isFinite(st)&&Number.isFinite(en)&&st<=now&&now<en;});
    const idx=current?timeline.indexOf(current):Math.max(0,timeline.length-1);
    const order=['Ketu','Venus','Sun','Moon','Mars','Rahu','Jupiter','Saturn','Mercury'];
    const durations=[7,20,6,10,7,18,16,19,17];
    const makeChildren=(start,end,lord,depth)=>{
      if(depth>5)return [];
      const st=Date.parse(start),en=Date.parse(end),total=en-st;
      if(!Number.isFinite(st)||!Number.isFinite(en)||total<=0)return [];
      const parentIndex=Math.max(0,order.indexOf(lord));
      let cur=st; const out=[];
      for(let k=0;k<9;k++){
        const i=(parentIndex+k)%9, child=order[i];
        const childEnd=k===8?en:cur+total*(durations[i]/120);
        const node={level:depth,level_name:{2:'Antardasha',3:'Pratyantar Dasha',4:'Sookshma Dasha',5:'Prana Dasha'}[depth]||'Dasha',lord:child,start:new Date(cur).toISOString(),end:new Date(childEnd).toISOString()};
        if(depth<5)node.children=makeChildren(node.start,node.end,child,depth+1);
        out.push(node); cur=childEnd;
      }
      return out;
    };
    const serialize=x=>({level:1,level_name:'Mahadasha',lord:x.lord,start:x.start,end:x.end,children:makeChildren(x.start,x.end,x.lord,2)});
    const selected=timeline.slice(Math.max(0,idx-2),idx+4).map(serialize);
    ctx.selected_mahadashas=selected;
    ctx.previous_mahadashas=selected.slice(0,Math.min(2,selected.length));
    ctx.current_mahadasha=selected[2]||selected[0]||null;
    ctx.next_mahadashas=selected.slice(3);
  }
  const selected=ctx.selected_mahadashas||[];
  const levelName={1:'Mahadasha',2:'Antardasha',3:'Pratyantar Dasha',4:'Sookshma Dasha',5:'Prana Dasha'};
  const nodeHtml=(node,depth=1)=>`<details class="dasha-node" ${depth===1?'open':''}><summary><span class="dasha-level">${esc(levelName[depth]||node.level_name||`Level ${depth}`)}</span><b>${esc(node.lord)}</b><span>${esc(String(node.start||'').slice(0,10))} → ${esc(String(node.end||'').slice(0,10))}</span></summary>${depth<5&&(node.children||[]).length?`<div class="dasha-children">${node.children.map(x=>nodeHtml(x,depth+1)).join('')}</div>`:''}</details>`;
  const labels=selected.map((x,i)=>{const phase=i<ctx.previous_mahadashas?.length?'Previous':(ctx.current_mahadasha?.start===x.start?'Current':'Next');return `<span>${phase}: ${esc(x.lord)} · ${esc(String(x.start||'').slice(0,10))} → ${esc(String(x.end||'').slice(0,10))}</span>`}).join('');
  $('personal-dashas').innerHTML=`<div class="section"><div class="section-heading"><div><span class="eyebrow">VIMSHOTTARI TIMING</span><h3>Mahadasha → Antardasha → Pratyantar → Sookshma → Prana</h3><p class="muted">Current Mahadasha plus the previous two and next three are shown. Expand any Mahadasha to inspect all available sub-periods.</p></div></div><div class="dasha-summary">${labels}</div><div class="dasha-tree">${selected.map(x=>nodeHtml(x,1)).join('')}</div></div>`;
}
function renderYogas(source){
  const c=source?.chart||source||{};
  const narrative=source?.narrative||c.narrative||{};
  const ys=narrative?.interpretations?.yoga_interpretations||[];
  const ds=narrative?.interpretations?.dosha_interpretations||[];
  // If an older/cached narrative is missing, use the chart-level detected Yoga/Dosha
  // objects rather than incorrectly reporting that no screen fired.
  const yogaRows=ys.length?ys:(c.yogas||[]).map(y=>({name:y.name,strength:y.strength,basis:y.basis,meaning:y.meaning||y.interpretation||y.basis,life_impact:y.life_impact||'',interpretation:y.interpretation||y.basis,fruitification:y.fruitification||{}}));
  const doshaRows=ds.length?ds:(c.doshas||[]).map(d=>({name:d.name,severity:d.severity,life_impact:d.life_impact||d.interpretation||d.basis,interpretation:d.interpretation||d.basis,basis:d.basis}));
  const yhtml=yogaRows.length?yogaRows.map(y=>{const impact=y.life_impact||y.interpretation||y.basis||''; const meaning=y.meaning||'Plain-language reading is not available for this yoga.'; const domains=(y.life_impact_by_domain||[]).map(z=>`<span>${esc(titleCase(z.domain))}: ${esc(z.text)}</span>`).join(''); return `<div class="card-line yoga-card"><div class="interpret-head"><b>${esc(y.name)}</b><span>${esc(y.strength||'')}</span></div><p><b>Meaning in plain language:</b> ${esc(meaning)}</p><p><b>Life Impact:</b> ${esc(impact)}</p>${domains?`<div class="mini-list yoga-domains">${domains}</div>`:''}<p><b>When Activated:</b> ${esc(y.interpretation||'')}</p>${(y.fruitification?.possible_dates||[]).slice(0,5).map(w=>`<small>Fruitification: <b>${esc(w.date)}</b> · ${esc(w.active_dasha||w.dasha_lord||'—')} · ${esc((w.transit_triggers||[]).map(a=>a.transit+'→'+a.natal).join(', ')||'transit contact')}</small>`).join('')}</div>`}).join(''):'<p class="muted">No applicable yoga screen fired for this chart.</p>';
  const dhtml=doshaRows.length?doshaRows.map(d=>`<div class="card-line"><div class="interpret-head"><b>${esc(d.name)}</b><span>${esc(d.severity||'')}</span></div><p><b>Life Impact:</b> ${esc(d.life_impact||d.interpretation||d.basis||'')}</p><p><b>When Activated:</b> ${esc(d.interpretation||d.basis||'')}</p></div>`).join(''):'<p class="muted">No applicable dosha screen fired for this chart.</p>';
  $('personal-yogas').innerHTML=`<div class="section"><h3>Applicable Yogas</h3><p class="muted">The Meaning field explains the yoga as a life pattern, not merely as a list of planetary placements.</p>${yhtml}</div><div class="section"><h3>Applicable Doshas / Afflictions</h3>${dhtml}</div>`;
}
function renderStrength(c){const ps=Object.entries(c.planet_strengths.planets).map(([p,d])=>row([esc(p+(d.components.retrograde?' (R)':'')),d.score,esc(d.components.dignity),d.components.house,d.components.combust?'Yes':'No'])).join('');const bs=c.bhava_strengths.map(h=>row([h.house,esc(h.sign),esc(h.lord),h.strength,esc((h.occupants||[]).join(', '))])).join('');$('personal-strength').innerHTML=`<div class="section"><h3>Planet strength screen</h3><p class="muted">Normalized 0–100 readability layer; not classical Shadbala units.</p><div class="table-wrap"><table>${th(['Planet','Score','Dignity','House','Combust'])}${ps}</table></div></div><div class="section"><h3>Bhava strength screen</h3><div class="table-wrap"><table>${th(['House','Sign','Lord','Strength','Occupants'])}${bs}</table></div></div>`}
function lineChart(rows,keys){
  if(!rows?.length)return '<div class="empty">No forecast rows.</div>';
  const W=1000,H=320,P=44; const xs=i=>P+i*(W-2*P)/Math.max(1,rows.length-1); const ys=v=>H-P-v*(H-2*P)/100;
  const palette=['#69d9ea','#b8a1ff','#f08fa9','#8fe1b1','#f1c46d','#8cb7ff','#d77cf2','#77e0c1','#ff9d76'];
  let s=`<svg class="modern-chart" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}"><rect width="${W}" height="${H}" rx="16" fill="#0b1118"/>`;
  [0,25,50,75,100].forEach(v=>s+=`<line x1="${P}" y1="${ys(v)}" x2="${W-P}" y2="${ys(v)}" stroke="#22303e"/><text x="8" y="${ys(v)+4}" fill="#8090a0" font-size="10">${v}</text>`);
  keys.forEach((k,idx)=>{const path=rows.map((r,i)=>(i?'L':'M')+xs(i).toFixed(1)+','+ys(Number(r[k]||0)).toFixed(1)).join(' ');s+=`<path d="${path}" fill="none" stroke="${palette[idx%palette.length]}" stroke-width="2.2"/>`});
  const step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(rows.length/8)); for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i+=step)s+=`<text x="${xs(i)}" y="${H-12}" text-anchor="middle" fill="#8090a0" font-size="9">${esc(rows[i].date.slice(0,10))}</text>`;
  return s+'</svg>';
}
function chartLegend(keys){const palette=['#69d9ea','#b8a1ff','#f08fa9','#8fe1b1','#f1c46d','#8cb7ff','#d77cf2','#77e0c1','#ff9d76']; return `<div class="chart-legend">${keys.map((k,i)=>`<span><i style="background:${palette[i%palette.length]}"></i>${esc(titleCase(k))}</span>`).join('')}</div>`;}

function renderTiming(c){
  const f=c.narrative?.forecast_interpretations||{};
  const rows=c.performance?.monthly||[];
  const domains=c.performance?.domains||[];
  const peaks=domains.map(d=>`<div class="peak-panel"><b>${esc(titleCase(d))}</b>${(c.performance.peaks[d]||[]).slice(0,5).map(x=>`<div class="peak-row"><b>${esc(x.date)}</b><span>${x.score}/100</span><small>${esc(x.dasha)}</small></div>`).join('')}</div>`).join('');
  const renderRows=(arr)=>`<div class="forecast-interpret-list">${(arr||[]).slice(0,320).map(x=>`<details class="forecast-row"><summary><b>${esc(x.date||x.year)}</b><span>${esc((x.focus||[]).join(' · '))}</span><em>${esc(x.dasha||'')}</em></summary><p><b>Interpretation:</b> ${esc(x.interpretation||'')}</p>${x.interpretation_depth?`<div class="interpretation-depth"><p><b>Summary:</b> ${esc(x.interpretation_depth.summary||'')}</p><p><b>Mechanism:</b> ${esc(x.interpretation_depth.mechanism||'')}</p><p><b>Cross-confirmation:</b> ${esc(x.interpretation_depth.cross_confirmation||'')}</p></div>`:''}${(x.deep_rules||[]).length?`<p><b>Additional corpus matches:</b> ${esc(x.deep_rules.join(' · '))}</p>`:''}</details>`).join('')}</div>`;
  $('personal-timing').innerHTML=`<div class="section"><div class="section-heading"><div><span class="eyebrow">DYNAMIC FORECAST</span><h3>${esc(c.forecast.amount)} ${esc(c.forecast.unit)} · ${esc(c.forecast.granularity)}</h3><p class="muted">${esc(c.forecast.start)} → ${esc(c.forecast.end)}</p></div></div><div class="chart-card dark-chart">${lineChart(rows,domains)}${chartLegend(domains)}<p class="chart-note"><b>How to read the colours:</b> each colour is one life-area activation series. Higher values mean the configured Jyotish timing rules are more strongly activated for that domain on that date. The colour identifies the life area; the score is a deterministic rule output.</p></div></div><div class="section"><h3>Peak activation windows</h3><div class="peak-grid">${peaks}</div></div><div class="section"><h3>Week-by-week interpretation</h3>${renderRows(f.weekly)}</div><div class="section"><h3>Month-by-month interpretation</h3>${renderRows(f.monthly)}</div><div class="section"><h3>Year-by-year interpretation</h3>${renderRows(f.yearly)}</div>`;
}
function renderPredictions(x){
  const c=x.chart||{}, p=x.narrative?.predictions||{};
  const rows=(p.predictions||[]);
  const methods=(p.methods||[]).map(m=>`<span class="method-chip">${esc(m)}</span>`).join('');
  const summary=(p.domain_summary||[]).map(z=>`<article class="prediction-card"><div class="interpret-head"><b>${esc(z.domain.replaceAll('_',' '))}</b><span>${esc(z.date)} · ${esc(String(z.score))}</span></div><p>${esc(z.interpretation)}</p></article>`).join('');
  const cards=rows.map(z=>{const dp=(z.domain_predictions||[]).slice(0,20).map(d=>`<div class="prediction-domain"><div class="interpret-head"><b>${esc(titleCase(d.domain))}</b><span>${esc(String(d.score))}/100 · ${esc(d.strength)}</span></div><p>${esc(d.prediction)}</p></div>`).join(''); const dv=(z.divisional_evidence||[]).map(v=>`<div class="prediction-varga"><b>${esc(v.varga)} · ${esc(v.purpose)}</b><p>${esc(v.text)}</p></div>`).join(''); return `<details class="forecast-row prediction-row"><summary><b>${esc(z.date)}</b><span>${esc((z.focus||[]).join(' · ')||'Integrated activation')}</span><em>${esc(z.dasha||'—')}</em></summary><div class="prediction-detail"><p class="direct-reading"><b>Direct result:</b> ${esc(z.interpretation||'')}</p><div class="prediction-domain-grid">${dp}</div>${dv?`<div class="prediction-varga-grid">${dv}</div>`:''}<div class="mini-list"><span>Prediction mode: ${esc(z.prediction_mode||'deterministic-rule')}</span><span>Evidence depth: ${esc(z.evidence_depth||'—')}</span><span>Jupiter H${esc(z.transit_houses?.Jupiter??'—')} · ${(z.transit_nakshatra?.Jupiter?.name||'—')} P${esc(z.transit_nakshatra?.Jupiter?.pada??'—')}</span><span>Saturn H${esc(z.transit_houses?.Saturn??'—')} · ${(z.transit_nakshatra?.Saturn?.name||'—')} P${esc(z.transit_nakshatra?.Saturn?.pada??'—')}</span><span>Rahu H${esc(z.transit_houses?.Rahu??'—')} · Ketu H${esc(z.transit_houses?.Ketu??'—')}</span>${(z.evidence||[]).slice(0,12).map(e=>`<span>${esc(e.type||'Evidence')}: ${esc(e.planet||e.system||e.name||e.value||'')}</span>`).join('')}</div></div></details>`}).join('');
  $('personal-predictions').innerHTML=`<div class="section prediction-hero"><span class="eyebrow">ADVANCED PREDICTIVE ASTROLOGY</span><h3>${esc(p.horizon?.amount||'')} ${esc(p.horizon?.unit||'')} · ${esc(p.granularity||'monthly')}</h3><p class="reading-paragraph">This section synthesizes the selected forecast horizon using the active Vimshottari hierarchy, slow-planet transits, transit Nakshatra/Pada, Ashtakavarga, alternative Dasha layers, Sudarshana and exact astronomical events available in the calculated horizon.</p><div class="method-chips">${methods}</div></div><div class="section"><h3>Strongest calculated life-area periods</h3><div class="interpret-grid">${summary||'<p class="muted">No domain summary is available for this horizon.</p>'}</div></div><div class="section"><h3>${esc((p.granularity||'forecast').replace(/(^|\s)\S/g,m=>m.toUpperCase()))}-by-${esc((p.granularity||'forecast'))} predictions</h3><p class="muted">${esc(p.count||0)} calculated periods are available. Expand a period to see the Dasha, transit, Nakshatra/Pada and advanced timing evidence behind the interpretation.</p><div class="forecast-interpret-list">${cards||'<p class="muted">No calculated prediction rows are available.</p>'}</div></div>`;
}
