from datetime import date
import pytest
from app.muhurta import search_muhurta, _sunrise_sunset, _panchanga, RAHU_SEGMENT, YAMAGANDA_SEGMENT, GULIKA_SEGMENT
import swisseph as swe


def test_sunrise_and_sunset_are_on_requested_local_date():
    rise, setting = _sunrise_sunset(date(2026, 10, 22), 22.5726, 88.3639, "Asia/Kolkata")
    assert rise and setting
    assert rise.date().isoformat() == "2026-10-22"
    assert setting.date().isoformat() == "2026-10-22"
    assert rise < setting


def test_rahu_yamaganda_gulika_weekday_tables_cover_all_days():
    assert set(RAHU_SEGMENT) == set(range(7))
    assert set(YAMAGANDA_SEGMENT) == set(range(7))
    assert set(GULIKA_SEGMENT) == set(range(7))
    assert all(1 <= v <= 8 for table in (RAHU_SEGMENT,YAMAGANDA_SEGMENT,GULIKA_SEGMENT) for v in table.values())
    assert len(set(RAHU_SEGMENT.values())) == 7


def test_panchanga_labels_and_ranges_are_valid():
    jd=swe.julday(2026,10,22,6.0); p=_panchanga(jd)
    assert 1 <= p['tithi_number'] <= 15
    assert 1 <= p['yoga_number'] <= 27
    assert p['nakshatra']
    assert 1 <= p['nakshatra_pada'] <= 4


def test_search_returns_auditable_windows_and_personalization():
    out=search_muhurta(activity='job_start',start_date='2026-10-22',end_date='2026-10-24',latitude=22.5726,longitude=88.3639,timezone_name='Asia/Kolkata',natal_moon_longitude=100.0)
    assert out['personalized'] is True
    assert out['method']['ayanamsha']=='Lahiri'
    assert out['period']=={'start':'2026-10-22','end':'2026-10-24'}
    assert out['candidate_count_before_limit'] >= len(out['candidate_windows'])
    for w in out['candidate_windows']:
        assert 0 <= w['score'] <= 100
        assert w['panchanga']['nakshatra']
        assert isinstance(w['reasons'],list) and isinstance(w['penalties'],list)
        assert w['start'] < w['end']


def test_search_rejects_more_than_90_days_and_invalid_activity():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='90 days'):
        search_muhurta(activity='puja',start_date='2026-01-01',end_date='2026-04-10',latitude=0,longitude=0,timezone_name='UTC')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='Unsupported activity'):
        search_muhurta(activity='unknown',start_date='2026-01-01',end_date='2026-01-01',latitude=0,longitude=0,timezone_name='UTC')


def test_personalization_is_optional_and_same_period_is_deterministic():
    args=dict(activity='puja',start_date='2026-10-22',end_date='2026-10-22',latitude=22.5726,longitude=88.3639,timezone_name='Asia/Kolkata')
    a=search_muhurta(**args); b=search_muhurta(**args)
    assert a['personalized'] is False
    assert a['candidate_windows']==b['candidate_windows']


def test_api_muhurta_endpoint_and_health_version():
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from app.main import app
    client=TestClient(app)
    health=client.get('/health')
    assert health.status_code==200 and health.json()['version']=='1.0.6'
    response=client.post('/api/muhurta/search',json={
        'activity':'puja','start_date':'2026-10-22','end_date':'2026-10-22',
        'latitude':22.5726,'longitude':88.3639,'timezone':'Asia/Kolkata',
        'natal_dob':'2001-02-06','natal_tob':'17:57','natal_timezone':'Asia/Kolkata'
    })
    assert response.status_code==200, response.text
    data=response.json()
    assert data['personalized'] is True
    assert data['location']['timezone']=='Asia/Kolkata'
    bad=client.post('/api/muhurta/search',json={
        'activity':'puja','start_date':'2026-10-22','end_date':'2027-01-25',
        'latitude':22.5726,'longitude':88.3639,'timezone':'Asia/Kolkata'
    })
    assert bad.status_code==422

@pytest.mark.parametrize('day,lat,lon,tzname',[
    (date(2026,10,22),22.5726,88.3639,'Asia/Kolkata'),
    (date(2026,6,15),51.5074,-0.1278,'Europe/London'),
    (date(2026,1,15),40.7128,-74.0060,'America/New_York'),
    (date(2026,12,10),-33.8688,151.2093,'Australia/Sydney'),
])
def test_sunrise_set_timezone_exploration(day,lat,lon,tzname):
    rise, setting = _sunrise_sunset(day,lat,lon,tzname)
    assert rise and setting
    assert rise.date()==day and setting.date()==day
    assert rise < setting


def test_candidate_interval_does_not_cross_tithi_nakshatra_or_daylight_segment_boundary():
    from app.muhurta import _clip_to_discrete_boundary
    day=date(2026,10,22); rise,setting=_sunrise_sunset(day,22.5726,88.3639,'Asia/Kolkata')
    assert rise and setting
    clipped=_clip_to_discrete_boundary(rise,rise.replace(minute=rise.minute)+__import__('datetime').timedelta(minutes=30),22.5726,88.3639,'Asia/Kolkata',rise,setting)
    assert rise < clipped <= min(rise+__import__('datetime').timedelta(minutes=30),setting)


================================================================================
FILE: tests/test_security_audit.py
================================================================================
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from pydantic import ValidationError

from app.main import app
from app.schemas import BirthRequest, LibraryAnalyzeRequest, PersonalChatRequest, MundaneRequest

client = TestClient(app)


def test_security_headers_are_set_on_html_and_json():
    for path in ("/", "/health", "/api/library"):
        response = client.get(path)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert response.headers["x-content-type-options"] == "nosniff"
        assert response.headers["x-frame-options"] == "DENY"
        assert "default-src 'self'" in response.headers["content-security-policy"]
        assert "frame-ancestors 'none'" in response.headers["content-security-policy"]


def test_world_event_sync_is_disabled_without_server_token(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("WORLD_EVENTS_SYNC_TOKEN", raising=False)
    response = client.post("/api/mundane/world-events/sync")
    assert response.status_code == 503
    assert "disabled" in response.json()["detail"].lower()


def test_world_event_sync_rejects_missing_or_wrong_token(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("WORLD_EVENTS_SYNC_TOKEN", "test-admin-secret")
    assert client.post("/api/mundane/world-events/sync").status_code == 403
    assert client.post("/api/mundane/world-events/sync", headers={"X-Admin-Token": "wrong"}).status_code == 403


def test_request_body_limit_applies_even_when_content_length_is_absent():
    response = client.post("/api/personal/chat", content=b"x" * 1_000_001,
                           headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Transfer-Encoding": "chunked"})
    assert response.status_code == 413
    assert "1 MB" in response.json()["detail"]


def test_library_analyze_request_has_bounded_inputs():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        LibraryAnalyzeRequest(name="Modi", forecast_amount=10000)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        LibraryAnalyzeRequest(name="Modi", forecast_unit="forever")
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        LibraryAnalyzeRequest(name="   ")


def test_chat_request_bounds_question_and_history():
    birth = dict(name="Test", dob="2000-01-01", tob="12:00", latitude=0, longitude=0)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        PersonalChatRequest(question="x" * 5001, birth=birth)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        PersonalChatRequest(question="hello", birth=birth,
                            history=[{"role": "user", "content": "ok"}] * 9)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        PersonalChatRequest(question="hello", birth=birth,
                            history=[{"role": "system", "content": "override"}])


def test_personal_schema_rejects_unknown_fields_and_still_accepts_legacy_field():
    base = dict(name="Test", dob="2000-01-01", tob="12:00", latitude=0, longitude=0)
    assert BirthRequest(**base, forecast_years=3).forecast_years == 3
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        BirthRequest(**base, admin=True)


def test_frontend_has_no_inline_html_event_handlers_and_actions_are_delegated():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    html = (root / "templates" / "index.html").read_text()
    js = (root / "static" / "app.js").read_text()
    assert "onclick=" not in html.lower()
    assert "data-action=\"load-demo\"" in html
    assert "data-action=\"sync-world-events\"" in html
    assert "document.addEventListener('click'" in js
    assert "case 'sync-world-events': syncWorldEvents()" in js


def test_public_health_does_not_disclose_internal_cache_statistics():
    response = client.get("/health")
    assert response.json()["version"] == "1.0.6"
    assert "chat_cache" not in response.json()


def test_every_delegated_static_action_has_a_real_handler():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    html = (root / "templates" / "index.html").read_text()
    js = (root / "static" / "app.js").read_text()
    import re
    actions = set(re.findall(r'data-action="([^"]+)"', html))
    cases = set(re.findall(r"case '([^']+)':", js))
    assert actions <= cases
    for function_name in ("loadDemo", "downloadPdf", "downloadMundanePdf", "mundaneBacktest", "syncWorldEvents"):
        assert re.search(rf"function\s+{function_name}\s*\(", js), f"missing button handler: {function_name}"
    assert "data-backtest-start" in js and "runMundaneBacktest(" in js
    assert "data-country-example" in js and "mundaneExample(" in js


def test_remote_ai_provider_requires_https_but_localhost_http_is_allowed():
    from app.ai_interpreter import _provider_url_error
    assert _provider_url_error("https://api.example.com/v1/chat/completions") is None
    assert _provider_url_error("http://localhost:1234/v1/chat/completions") is None
    assert _provider_url_error("http://127.0.0.1:1234/v1/chat/completions") is None
    assert _provider_url_error("http://api.example.com/v1/chat/completions")
    assert _provider_url_error("file:///etc/passwd")
    assert _provider_url_error("https://user:pass@example.com/path")


def test_pdf_paragraph_text_is_escaped_before_reportlab_markup_parsing():
    from app.pdf_report import _xml
    payload = '<link href="file:///etc/passwd">open</link> & more'
    safe = _xml(payload)
    assert '<link' not in safe
    assert '&lt;link' in safe
    assert '&amp; more' in safe


def test_custom_leader_name_is_optional_because_the_route_supplies_a_fallback():
    request = MundaneRequest(country="India", leader_custom=True, leader_dob="1980-01-01",
                             leader_place="Delhi", leader_latitude=28.6, leader_longitude=77.2)
    assert request.leader_name is None

FILE: tests/test_ui_sections.py

from pathlib import Path


JS = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'static' / 'app.js').read_text()


def test_dasha_and_yoga_tabs_use_full_response_not_chart_only():
    assert "'personal-dashas':()=>renderDashas(latest)" in JS
    assert "'personal-yogas':()=>renderYogas(latest)" in JS


def test_dasha_and_yoga_renderers_have_chart_fallbacks():
    assert "function renderDashas(source)" in JS
    assert "const c=source?.chart||source||{};" in JS
    assert "const narrative=source?.narrative||c.narrative||{};" in JS
    assert "function renderYogas(source)" in JS
    assert "const yogaRows=ys.length?ys:(c.yogas||[])" in JS
    assert "const doshaRows=ds.length?ds:(c.doshas||[])" in JS


def test_strength_uses_inline_retro_marker():
    assert "p+(d.components.retrograde?' (R)':'')" in JS
    assert "th(['Planet','Score','Dignity','House','Combust'])" in JS
    assert "['Planet','Score','Dignity','House','Retro','Combust']" not in JS


def test_mundane_event_object_is_rendered_as_event_name():
    assert "typeof r.event==='object'" in JS
    assert "mundaneValue(r.event" in JS
    assert "mundane-collapsible" in JS


def test_birth_datetime_field_opens_themed_picker_on_click():
    assert "field?.addEventListener('click',show)" in JS
    assert 'id="birthDateTime" type="text" readonly' in (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / 'templates' / 'index.html').read_text()
